\displaystyle\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\rangle\leq\frac{n}{d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\beta_{n}\left(\frac{\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}}{\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}},\frac{\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{*}}{\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}}\right).

Under
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{4}
, we have for any
{\mathcal{A}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\ldots\times d_{m}}
,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\beta_{n}\left(\frac{\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}}{\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\rm{F}}},\frac{\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{*}}{\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\rm{F}}}\right)\lesssim_{m}\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{*}

\displaystyle\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})

\displaystyle+\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\frac{n\log(\overline{d})}{d^{*}}}+\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}^{2}\log(\overline{d}).

And this implies that

\displaystyle\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{A}}\rangle\leq\frac{n}{d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C_{m}\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{*}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d}).

(13)

Now we focus on
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}},\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{*}
.

Estimation of
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
.
Notice that we have
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu
and
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\leq\kappa_{0}\nu
from Lemma
2
. Using triangle inequality, we have

\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
.
Meanwhile, for all
1\leq i\leq m-1
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}(\Lambda_{i+1})\leq\sigma_{\max}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})+\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{11}{10}\overline{\sigma}.

(14)

As a result of the above inequality and Lemma
21
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}=\|{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}=\|T^{\leq i}\Lambda_{i+1}V_{i+1}^{T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\frac{11}{10}\overline{\sigma}\|T^{\leq i}\|_{2,\infty}\|V_{i+1}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\frac{22}{5}\overline{\sigma}\frac{\kappa_{0}^{4}\nu^{2}r_{i}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}.

As this holds for all
i\in[m-1]
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\frac{22}{5}\frac{\underline{r}\kappa_{0}^{4}\nu^{2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\overline{\sigma}.

(15)

On the other hand, we have
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{T}}=\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{1}+\ldots+\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{m}
, where

\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{i}=[T_{1}^{*},\ldots,T_{i-1}^{*},X_{i},T_{i+1}^{*},\ldots,T_{m}^{*}]

and the expression of
X_{i}
are give in (
4.3
). Now we would like to estimate
\|\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
. Since the reshape operation remains the infinity norm unchanged, we have for all
i\in[m-1]
,

\displaystyle\|\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{i}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle=\|\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{i}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle=\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}+\|T^{*\leq i}T^{*\leq iT}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}},

and for
i=m
,

\displaystyle\|\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{m}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle=\|\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{m}^{\langle m\rangle}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}=\|(T^{*\leq m-1}\otimes I)(T^{*\leq m-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle m\rangle}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{Lemma}~\ref{lemma:reshape}}{=}\|T^{*\leq m-1}T^{*\leq m-1T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}.

We check the term
\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
.

\displaystyle~~~~\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sqrt{d_{i+1}\ldots d_{m}}\|(T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T}\otimes I){\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\|V_{i+1}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\|T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i-1\rangle}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\kappa_{0}\nu\sqrt{r_{i}},

(16)

where in
(a)
we use Lemma
21
and
\|V_{i+1}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}=\|V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\|_{2,\infty}
; in
(b)
we use Lemma
20
and
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\leq\kappa_{0}\nu
. Also, when
2\leq i\leq m

\displaystyle\|T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i-1\rangle}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle=\|T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T}T^{\leq i-1}\Lambda_{i}V_{i}^{T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\max}(\Lambda_{i})\|V_{i}\|_{2,\infty}\cdot\max_{j}\|T^{\leq i-1T}T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T}e_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sigma_{\max}(\Lambda_{i})\|V_{i}\|_{2,\infty}\|T^{\leq i-1}\|_{2,\infty}\|T^{*\leq i-1}\|_{2,\infty}\sqrt{d_{1}\ldots d_{i-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{22}{5}\frac{\kappa_{0}^{5}\nu^{3}r_{i-1}^{3/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\overline{\sigma},

where in
(a)
we use Lemma
21
and in
(b)
we use
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\leq\kappa_{0}\nu,\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu
and (
14
). And when
i=1
,

\displaystyle\|T^{*\leq 0}T^{*\leq 0T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle 0\rangle}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}=\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\overset{\eqref{estimation of infinity norm of T}}{\leq}\frac{22}{5}\frac{\underline{r}\kappa_{0}^{4}\nu^{2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\overline{\sigma}.

Combine these with (
A.1.1
) and we get

\displaystyle\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{rlc}&\frac{22}{5}\frac{\kappa_{0}^{5}\nu^{3}\underline{r}r_{1}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\overline{\sigma},\quad i=1\\
&\frac{22}{5}\frac{\kappa_{0}^{6}\nu^{4}r_{i-1}^{3/2}r_{i}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\overline{\sigma},\quad 2\leq i\leq m\end{array}\right.

\displaystyle\leq\frac{22}{5}\frac{\kappa_{0}^{6}\nu^{4}\overline{r}^{2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\overline{\sigma}.

Now for all
i\in[m-1]
, we check
\|T^{*\leq i}T^{*\leq iT}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}
.

\displaystyle\|T^{*\leq i}T^{*\leq iT}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle=\max_{j,k}|e_{j}^{T}T^{*\leq i}T^{*\leq iT}T^{\leq i}\Lambda_{i+1}V_{i+1}^{T}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}e_{k}|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{j,k}\sigma_{\max}(\Lambda_{i+1})\|T^{\leq iT}T^{*\leq i}T^{*\leq iT}e_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}\|V_{i+1}^{T}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}e_{k}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\max}(\Lambda_{i+1})\|T^{*\leq i}\|_{2,\infty}\|T^{\leq i}\|_{2,\infty}\|V_{i+1}\|_{2,\infty}\|V_{i+1}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\sqrt{d^{*}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{22}{5}\frac{\kappa_{0}^{6}\nu^{4}r_{i}^{2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\overline{\sigma}.

Put these together and we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\frac{44m}{5}\frac{\kappa_{0}^{6}\nu^{4}\overline{r}^{2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\overline{\sigma}.

(17)

Estimation of
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{*}
.
First notice
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}={\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{T}}
. From Lemma
24
and
\textsf{rank}_{\textsf{tt}}({\mathcal{T}})\leq(r_{1},\ldots,r_{m-1})
, we know
\textsf{rank}_{\textsf{tt}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}})\leq(3r_{1},\ldots,3r_{m-1})
. Now we use Lemma
25
and we get

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{*}\leq 3^{(m-1)/2}\sqrt{r_{1}\ldots r_{m-1}}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}.

(18)

Now we combine (
17
), (
18
), (
13
) and Lemma
27
, since
\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{20m}
,

\displaystyle\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\rangle\leq

\displaystyle 400m^{2}\frac{n}{d^{*}}\frac{\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{m}\frac{\kappa_{0}^{7}\nu^{4}\overline{r}^{2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\sqrt{r_{1}\ldots r_{m-1}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d}).

(19)

So as long as

n\geq C_{m}\left(\kappa_{0}^{7}\nu^{4}\sqrt{d^{*}}\overline{r}^{2}(r^{*})^{1/2}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})+\kappa_{0}^{14}\nu^{8}\overline{d}\overline{r}^{4}r^{*}\log^{2m+4}(\overline{d})\right)
,
we have from (
A.1.1
) and (
A.1.1
), we have

\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}\rangle\leq\frac{1}{100}\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\rangle.

Together with (
11
) and (
A.1.1
), we get

\displaystyle\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{T}},{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{T}}\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{49}{100}\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle\geq\frac{49}{100}(\frac{n}{2d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}\frac{200m^{2}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{6n}{25d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

(20)

where the last inequality holds since
\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{600m}\underline{\sigma}
.

A.1.2
Estimation of
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

First notice that we have both
{\mathcal{T}}
and
{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
are of TT rank
(r_{1},\ldots,r_{m-1})
. And
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu
,
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\leq\kappa_{0}\nu\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu=:\sqrt{\mu}
. First notice that

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 1001\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+1.001\frac{n^{2}}{(d^{*})^{2}}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(21)

Now we check
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}
. From the variational representation of Frobenius norm, we can write it as

\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}=\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{X}}_{0})\rangle,

for some
{\mathcal{X}}_{0}
with
\|{\mathcal{X}}_{0}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 1
. Now we set

{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{X}}_{0})=\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{1}+\ldots+\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{m}
, with
\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}=[T_{1},\ldots,X_{i},\ldots,T_{m}]
. For all
i\in[m]
, we consider the bound for
\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle
.
We can decompose
{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
as

\displaystyle{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}

\displaystyle=[T_{1}^{*},\ldots,\Delta_{i},\ldots,T_{m}^{*}]+\sum_{j=1}^{i-1}[T_{1}^{*},\ldots,\Delta_{j},\ldots,T_{i},T_{i+1}^{*},\ldots,T_{m}^{*}]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{j=i+1}^{m}[T_{1},\ldots,T_{i},T_{i+1},\ldots,\Delta_{j},\ldots,T_{m}^{*}]

\displaystyle=:{\mathcal{Y}}_{i,i}+\sum_{j=1}^{i-1}{\mathcal{Y}}_{i,j}+\sum_{j=i+1}^{m}{\mathcal{Y}}_{i,j},

(22)

where
\Delta_{j}=T_{j}-T_{j}^{*}
.
Before the estimation, we need the following lemmas whose proofs are presented in the Section
C
.

Lemma 13
.

Suppose that
\Omega
is the set sampled uniformly with replacement with size
|\Omega|=n
. Then under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
, we have for any tensors
{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{B}}
with TT rank
(r_{1},\ldots,r_{m-1})
,

\displaystyle~~~~|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{B}}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\cdot\prod_{i=1}^{m}\left(\max_{x_{i}}\|A_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}\cdot\|B_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\wedge\max_{x_{i}}\|B_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}\cdot\|A_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\right),

where
{\mathcal{A}}=[A_{1},\ldots,A_{m}]
and
{\mathcal{B}}=[B_{1},\ldots,B_{m}]
can be arbitrary decompositions such that
A_{i},B_{i}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{i-1}\times d_{i}\times r_{i}}
.

Lemma 14
.

Let
{\mathcal{T}}
be a tensor of rank
(r_{1},\ldots,r_{m-1})
such that
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}})\leq\sqrt{\mu}
,
and it has a left orthogonal decomposition
{\mathcal{T}}=[T_{1},\ldots,T_{m}]
.
Then we have

\max_{x_{i}}\|T_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{\mu r_{i}}{d_{i}},\quad\|T_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}=\sqrt{r_{i}},i\in[m-1],

\max_{x_{m}}\|T_{m}(:,x_{m})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\sigma_{\max}^{2}({\mathcal{T}})\frac{\mu r_{m-1}}{d_{m}},\quad\|T_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{\underline{r}}\sigma_{\max}({\mathcal{T}}).

Now we present some bounds related to
\Delta_{j}
and
X_{j}
.

Properties for
\Delta_{j}
.

For all
j\in[m-1]
, we estimate
\|\Delta_{j}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|L(T_{j})-L(T_{j}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}
as follows. Notice
{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle j\rangle}=(T^{\leq j-1}\otimes I)L(T_{j})T^{\geq j+1},({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle j\rangle}=(T^{*\leq j-1}\otimes I)L(T_{j}^{*})T^{*\geq j+1}
. So we have,

\displaystyle\|L(T_{j})-L(T_{j}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|(T^{\leq j-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle j\rangle}V_{j+1}\Lambda_{j+1}^{-1}-(T^{*\leq j-1}\otimes I)^{T}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle j\rangle}V_{j+1}^{*}(\Lambda_{j+1}^{*})^{-1}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|((T^{\leq j-1}\otimes I)^{T}-(T^{*\leq j-1}\otimes I)^{T}){\mathcal{T}}^{\langle j\rangle}V_{j+1}\Lambda_{j+1}^{-1}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\|(T^{*\leq j-1}\otimes I)^{T}({\mathcal{T}}^{\langle j\rangle}-({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle j\rangle})V_{j+1}\Lambda_{j+1}^{-1}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle~~~~+\|(T^{*\leq j-1}\otimes I)^{T}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle j\rangle}(V_{j+1}\Lambda_{j+1}^{-1}-V_{j+1}^{*}(\Lambda_{j+1}^{*})^{-1})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sigma_{\min}^{-1}({\mathcal{T}})\frac{\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{2\sigma_{\min}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})}\sqrt{r_{j}}\sigma_{\max}({\mathcal{T}})+\sigma_{\min}^{-1}({\mathcal{T}})\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\underline{r}}\sigma_{\max}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\frac{12\kappa_{0}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}20\sqrt{r_{j}}\frac{\kappa_{0}^{2}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})},

where in
(a)
we use (
10
) and the bound

\|V_{j+1}\Lambda_{j+1}^{-1}-V_{j+1}^{*}(\Lambda_{j+1}^{*})^{-1}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|(V_{j+1}-V_{j+1}^{*})(\Lambda_{j+1}^{*})^{-1}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|V_{j+1}(\Lambda_{j+1}^{-1}-(\Lambda_{j+1}^{*})^{-1})\|_{\rm{F}}

and Lemma
22
and in
(b)
we use
|\sigma_{\max}({\mathcal{T}})-\sigma_{\max}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})|\vee|\sigma_{\min}({\mathcal{T}})-\sigma_{\min}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})|\leq\frac{1}{10}\sigma_{\min}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})
. When
i=m
, we have

\displaystyle\|T_{m}-T_{m}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|T^{\leq m-1T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}-T^{*\leq m-1T}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|(T^{\leq m-1T}-T^{*\leq m-1T})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|T^{\leq m-1T}({\mathcal{T}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}-({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{r_{m-1}}\kappa_{0}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 3\sqrt{r_{m-1}}\kappa_{0}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{rlc}&20\sigma_{\min}^{-1}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\sqrt{r_{i}}\kappa_{0}^{2}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}},i\in[m-1]\\
&3\sqrt{r_{m-1}}\kappa_{0}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}},i=m.\end{array}\right.

On the other hand, from Lemma
14
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{x_{i}}\|\Delta_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{rlc}&2\sqrt{\mu r_{i}d_{i}^{-1}},i\in[m-1]\\
&2\sqrt{\mu r_{m-1}d_{m}^{-1}}\cdot\sigma_{\max}({\mathcal{T}}^{*}),i=m.\end{array}\right.

Upper bound for
\|X_{j}\|_{\rm{F}}
.
For all
X_{i},i\in[m-1]
, we have
L(X_{i})=(I-L(T_{i})L(T_{i})^{T})(T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}T^{\geq i+1T}(T^{\geq i+1}T^{\geq i+1T})^{-1}
, thus
\|X_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sigma_{\min}^{-1}({\mathcal{T}})\leq 2\sigma_{\min}^{-1}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})
. For
i=m
,
L(X_{m})=(T^{\leq m-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle m\rangle}
, so
\|X_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 1
. Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\|X_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 2\sigma_{\min}^{-1}({\mathcal{T}}^{*}),i\in[m-1],\quad\|X_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 1.

(27)

Now we consider the upper bound for
\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle
.

When
i\in[m-1]
.

Due to (
A.1.2
), we can write it as

\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{Y}}_{i,j},\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle.

Now for all
j\neq i,m
, we consider
\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,j}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle
.
By setting
{\mathcal{A}}={\mathcal{Y}}_{i,j}
,
{\mathcal{B}}=\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}
in Lemma
13
, together with Lemma
14
, (
A.1.2
), (
A.1.2
), (
27
) and we have under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
,

|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,j}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu^{m/2}\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}}{\sqrt{r_{i}d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

For
j=m
, we have under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
,

|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,m}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\kappa_{0}^{2}\mu^{m/2}\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}}{\sqrt{r_{i}d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

And when
j=i
, we estimate
|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,i}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle|
. We can write

\displaystyle\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}

\displaystyle=[T_{1}^{*},\ldots,X_{i},\ldots,T_{m}^{*}]+[\Delta_{1},T_{2},\ldots,X_{i},\ldots,T_{m}]+\ldots+[T_{1}^{*},\ldots,X_{i},T_{i+1}^{*},\ldots,\Delta_{m}]

\displaystyle=:{\mathcal{X}}_{i,0}+\sum_{k=1,k\neq i}^{m}{\mathcal{X}}_{i,k}.

(28)

Then
\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,i}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle=\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,i}),{\mathcal{X}}_{i,0}\rangle+\sum_{k=1,k\neq i}^{m}\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,i}),{\mathcal{X}}_{i,k}\rangle
. And here the first term can be bounded using Corollary
12
,

|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,i}),{\mathcal{X}}_{i,0}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{4}\frac{\mu\overline{r}\sqrt{n\overline{d}\log(\overline{d})}}{d^{*}}\sqrt{r_{i}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{4}\frac{\mu\overline{r}^{3/2}\sqrt{n\overline{d}\log(\overline{d})}}{d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

When
k\neq i,m
, we have

|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,i}),{\mathcal{X}}_{i,k}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\mu^{m/2}\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}}{\sqrt{r_{i}d^{*}}}\kappa_{0}^{4}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

When
k=m
, we have

|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,i}),{\mathcal{X}}_{i,m}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\mu^{m/2}\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}}{\sqrt{r_{i}d^{*}}}\kappa_{0}^{2}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

In summary, under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
, we have

|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{i,i}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu^{m/2}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}r_{i}^{-1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Together with the estimation when
j\neq i
, we see that under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
,

\displaystyle\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}\rangle\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu^{m/2}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}r_{i}^{-1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

When
i=m
.

Similarly, we write

\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{m}\rangle=\sum_{j=1}^{m}\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{Y}}_{m,j},\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{m}\rangle.

First when
j\in[m-1]
, we have

\displaystyle|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{m,j}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{m}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\kappa_{0}^{3}\mu^{m/2}\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

(29)

And when
j=m
, we consider
\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{m,m}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{m}\rangle=\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{m,m}),{\mathcal{X}}_{m,0}\rangle+\sum_{k=1}^{m-1}\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{m,m}),{\mathcal{X}}_{m,k}\rangle
as in (
A.1.2
). Similarly, using Lemma
11
, we have

\displaystyle\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{m,m}),{\mathcal{X}}_{m,0}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}\frac{\mu\overline{r}\sqrt{n\overline{d}\log(\overline{d})}}{d^{*}}\sqrt{r_{m-1}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}\frac{\mu\overline{r}^{3/2}\sqrt{n\overline{d}\log(\overline{d})}}{d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

And when
k\in[m-1]
, we have

\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{m,m}),{\mathcal{X}}_{m,k}\rangle\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{3}\mu^{m/2}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

So under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
, we have

|\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{Y}}_{m,m}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{m}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{3}\mu^{m/2}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}},

and

\displaystyle\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{m}\rangle\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{3}\mu^{m/2}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

(30)

Now we conclude from (
29
), (
30
) and under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu^{m/2}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\frac{r^{*}r_{m-1}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}r_{i}^{-1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Together with (
21
), as long as

n\geq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{8}\mu^{m}\log^{2m+4}(\overline{d})\cdot\overline{d}(r^{*})^{2}r_{m-1}^{2}(\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}r_{i}^{-1})+C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{4}\mu^{m/2}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\cdot(d^{*})^{1/2}r^{*}r_{m-1}(\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}r_{i}^{-1/2}),

we have under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 1.002\frac{n^{2}}{(d^{*})^{2}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(31)

A.1.3
Estimation of
\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\rangle

Now we derive the upper bound for
\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\rangle
. First we have

\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\rangle=\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\rangle+\frac{n}{d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

As in (
A.1.2
), we write
{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
as
{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\mathcal{Y}}_{m,j}
. Notice as estimated above (
18
), we have
\textsf{rank}_{\textsf{tt}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}))\leq(3r_{1},\ldots,3r_{m-1})
. So there exists a TT decomposition of
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})=[Y_{1},\ldots,Y_{m-1},Y_{m}]
such that
\|Y_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}=\sqrt{3r_{i}},i\in[m-1]
and
\|Y_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}
.

When
j\in[m-1]
, we estimate
\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{Y}}_{m,j}\rangle
. Using Lemma
13
, Lemma
14
, (
A.1.2
), (
A.1.2
), (
27
) and Lemma
27
, and we have under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
,

\displaystyle|\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{Y}}_{m,j}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\mu^{m/2}\kappa_{0}\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

And when
j=m
, we have under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
,

\displaystyle|\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{Y}}_{m,m}\rangle|\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\mu^{m/2}\kappa_{0}\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

So we conclude under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
,

\displaystyle~~~~|\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\mu^{m/2}\kappa_{0}\frac{r^{*}\cdot r_{m-1}^{1/2}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{n}{d^{*}}\frac{300m^{2}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{1000d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

(32)

where the last inequality holds as long as

n\geq C_{m}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\mu^{m/2}\kappa_{0}r^{*}\overline{r}^{1/2}\sqrt{d^{*}}+C_{m}\overline{d}\overline{r}(r^{*})^{2}\mu^{m}\kappa_{0}^{2}\log^{2m+4}(\overline{d}).

A.1.4
Contraction

Now we consider the error
\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l+1}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
assuming
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}
holds.
From Algorithm
2
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l+1}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}(\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}_{l})-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\overset{\text{Lemma}~\ref{lemma:tt-perturbation}}{\leq}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{600m\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\underline{\sigma}}.

(33)

From the way we choose
\zeta_{l}
, we have
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
. Now we estimate
\|{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\alpha_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-2\alpha_{l}\underbrace{\langle{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*},{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\rangle}_{\textup{I}}

\displaystyle\qquad+2\alpha_{l}\underbrace{\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}),{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\rangle}_{\textup{II}}+\alpha_{l}^{2}\underbrace{\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}_{\textup{III}}.

From (
A.1.1
),(
A.1.3
) and (
31
), when

\displaystyle n\geq C_{m}\bigg(\kappa_{0}^{2m+4}\nu^{m+1}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\cdot(d^{*})^{1/2}((r^{*})^{1/2}\overline{r}^{2}\vee r^{*}r_{m-1}\underline{r}^{-1/2}\vee r^{*}\overline{r}^{1/2})

\displaystyle+\kappa_{0}^{4m+8}\nu^{2m+2}\log^{2m+4}(\overline{d})\cdot\overline{d}(r^{*}\overline{r}^{4}\vee(r^{*})^{2}r^{2}_{m-1}\underline{r}^{-1}\vee(r^{*})^{2}\overline{r})\bigg),

we obtain

\textup{I}\geq\frac{6n}{25d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},\quad|\textup{II}|\leq\frac{n}{1000d^{*}}\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},\quad|\textup{III}|\leq 1.002\frac{n^{2}}{(d^{*})^{2}}\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

From these estimations and we get,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}(1-0.239\alpha_{l}\frac{n}{d^{*}}+1.002\alpha_{l}^{2}\frac{n^{2}}{(d^{*})^{2}}).

Now we set
\alpha_{l}=0.12\frac{d^{*}}{n}
, and we get
\|{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 0.986\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
.
When
\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{600000m}
, we have from (
33
)

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l+1}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 0.975\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

And this implies
\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l+1}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{600000m\kappa_{0}\sqrt{\overline{r}}}
and
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}_{l+1})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu
is implied by Lemma
29
. So we finish the proof of Lemma
9
.

A.2
Proof of Lemma
6

Before we start the proof, we give a detailed version of the theorem, notice here we set
|\Omega_{i}|=n_{i}
.

Lemma 15

(Restate of Lemma
6
)
.

Suppose the conditions of
{\mathcal{T}}^{\ast}
from Theorem
4
hold. For any absolute constant
C>0
, there exists an absolute constant
C_{m}>0
depending only on
m
such that if

\displaystyle n

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{2m-1}n_{i}\geq C_{m}\nu^{m+3}\kappa_{0}^{4m-4}\log^{2}(\overline{d})\bigg((d^{\ast})^{1/2}(r^{*}\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2})^{1/2}+\overline{d}r^{*}\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{m-2}\big((d_{k}\cdots d_{m})^{1/2}(r_{1}\cdots r_{k-1})^{3}r_{k}(r_{k+1}\cdots r_{m-1})^{1/2}(\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2})^{1/2}+\overline{d}(r_{1}\cdots r_{k-1})^{2}r^{*}\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+(\overline{d}r^{*}\underline{r}\overline{r}r_{m-1})^{1/2}+\overline{d}\overline{r}\underline{r}r_{m-1}+r^{*}\underline{r}\overline{r}r_{m-1}\bigg),

then with probability at least
1-m\overline{d}^{-m}
, the output of Algorithm
3
satisfies

\|{\mathcal{T}}_{0}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{Cm\kappa_{0}^{2}\overline{r}^{1/2}}\quad\text{~and~}\quad\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}_{0})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu.

Step 0:

We denote
R_{i}=\arg\min_{R\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r_{i}}}\|\widehat{T}^{\leq i}-T^{*\leq i}R\|_{\rm{F}}
and we set
\sqrt{\mu}=2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu
.

Step 1:
When
i=1
.

Firstly from Wedin’s sin
\Theta
theorem, and from Lemma
34
, we see that when
n_{1},n_{2}\geq C_{m}\nu^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2m-2}\cdot(d^{*})^{1/2}(r^{*}\overline{r})^{1/2}\underline{r}\log^{2}(\overline{d})+C_{m}\nu^{4}\kappa_{0}^{4m-4}\overline{d}r^{*}\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle d_{p}(\widetilde{T}_{1},T_{1}^{*})\leq\frac{2\sqrt{r_{1}}\|N_{1}-N_{1}^{*}\|}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}\leq(C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{2m-4}(r_{2}\cdots r_{m-1})^{1/2}\overline{r}^{1/2})^{-1}.

(34)

Now from Lemma
37
, we know
\textsf{Incoh}(\widehat{T}_{1})\leq\sqrt{3\mu}
and

d_{c}(\widehat{T}_{1},T_{1}^{*})\leq(C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{2m-4}(r_{2}\cdots r_{m-1})^{1/2}\overline{r}^{1/2})^{-1}
.

Step 2:
When
2\leq i\leq m-1
.

Suppose we already have
\textsf{Incoh}(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1})\leq(3\mu)^{(i-1)/2}(r_{1}\cdots r_{i-2})^{3/2}=:\sqrt{\mu_{i-1}}
and
d_{c}(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1},T^{*\leq i-1})\leq(C_{i-1}m^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2t_{i-1}}\sqrt{\overline{r}\cdot r_{i}})^{-1}
.
From Lemma
35
, we see that,

\displaystyle~~~~d_{p}(L(\widetilde{T}_{i}),(R_{i-1}\otimes I)^{T}L(T_{i}^{*}))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\sqrt{r_{i}}\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}N_{i}(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)-(T^{*\leq i-1}R_{i-1}\otimes I)^{T}N_{i}^{*}(T^{*\leq i-1}R_{i-1}\otimes I)\|}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}.

Notice that

\displaystyle~~~~\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}N_{i}(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)-(T^{*\leq i-1}R_{i-1}\otimes I)^{T}N_{i}^{*}(T^{*\leq i-1}R_{i-1}\otimes I)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}(N_{i}-N_{i}^{*})(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)\|+2\|\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}-T^{*\leq i-1}R_{i-1}\|\cdot\overline{\sigma}^{2}.

So we have

\displaystyle d_{p}(L(\widetilde{T}_{i}),(R_{i-1}\otimes I)^{T}L(T_{i}^{*}))

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{r_{i}}\underline{\sigma}^{-2}\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}(N_{i}-N_{i}^{*})(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)\|

\displaystyle\qquad+4\sqrt{r_{i}}\kappa_{0}^{2}d_{c}(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1},T^{*\leq i-1})

\displaystyle=:b_{i-1}+4\sqrt{r_{i}}\kappa_{0}^{2}x_{i-1}.

(35)

Now we derive the chordal distance between
\widehat{T}^{\leq i}
and
T^{*\leq i}
. Notice that

\displaystyle d_{c}(\widehat{T}^{\leq i},T^{*\leq i})

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r_{i}}d_{c}(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1},T^{*\leq i-1})+d_{c}(L(\widehat{T}_{i}),(R_{i-1}\otimes I)^{T}L(T_{i}^{*}))

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r_{i}}d_{c}(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1},T^{*\leq i-1})+\sqrt{2}d_{p}(L(\widehat{T}_{i}),(R_{i-1}\otimes I)^{T}L(T_{i}^{*}))

\displaystyle\overset{\text{Lemma~}\ref{lemma:trim}}{\leq}\sqrt{r_{i}}d_{c}(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1},T^{*\leq i-1})+4\sqrt{2}\pi\cdot d_{p}(L(\widetilde{T}_{i}),(R_{i-1}\otimes I)^{T}L(T_{i}^{*}))

Together with (
A.2
), and denote by
q_{i-1}=80\sqrt{r_{i}}\kappa_{0}^{2}
, we have
x_{i}\leq q_{i-1}x_{i-1}+b_{i-1}
. Sum this up and we have

x_{m-1}\leq q_{m-1}\cdots q_{1}x_{1}+\sum_{k=1}^{m-2}q_{m-2}\cdots q_{k+1}b_{k}.

From Lemma
35
, as long as

\displaystyle n_{2k+1},n_{2k+2}\geq C_{m}\nu^{k+2}\kappa_{0}^{2m}\log^{2}(\overline{d})\cdot(d_{k}\cdots d_{m})^{1/2}(r_{1}\cdots r_{k-1})^{3}r_{k}(r_{k+1}\cdots r_{m-1})^{1/2}(\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2})^{1/2}

\displaystyle+C_{m}\nu^{k+4}\kappa_{0}^{4m-2k}\log(\overline{d})\cdot\overline{d}(r_{1}\cdots r_{k-1})^{3}r_{k}r_{k+1}\cdots r_{m-1}(\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2}),

we have

q_{m-2}\cdots q_{k+1}b_{k}\leq\frac{1}{Cm^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\overline{r}}}
. Together with the estimation in Step 1, we have

\displaystyle d_{c}(\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1},T^{*\leq m-1})=x_{m-1}\leq(Cm\kappa_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\overline{r}})^{-1}.

(36)

Step 3:
When
i=m
.
We have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\widehat{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|T^{*\leq m-1}R_{m-1}R_{m-1}^{T}T_{m}^{*}-\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1}\widehat{T}_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|(T^{*\leq m-1}R_{m-1}-\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})R_{m-1}^{T}T_{m}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|R_{m-1}^{T}T_{m}^{*}-\widehat{T}_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq d_{c}(T^{*\leq m-1},\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})\cdot\overline{\sigma}+\|R_{m-1}^{T}T_{m}^{*}-\widehat{T}_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Notice
d_{c}(T^{*\leq m-1},\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})
is estimated in
(
36
)
. On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\|R_{m-1}^{T}T_{m}^{*}-\widehat{T}_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|(T^{*\leq m-1}R_{m-1})^{T}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}-(\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})^{T}(\frac{d^{*}}{n_{2m-1}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{2m-1}}({\mathcal{T}}^{*}))^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})^{T}(({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}-(\frac{d^{*}}{n_{2m-1}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{2m-1}}({\mathcal{T}}^{*}))^{\langle m-1\rangle})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|(T^{*\leq m-1}R_{m-1}-\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})^{T}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})^{T}(({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}-(\frac{d^{*}}{n_{2m-1}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{2m-1}}({\mathcal{T}}^{*}))^{\langle m-1\rangle})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\qquad+d_{c}(T^{*\leq m-1},\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})\cdot\overline{\sigma}.

(37)

Together with Lemma
36
, we have as long as

\displaystyle n_{2m-1}

\displaystyle\geq C_{m}\nu^{(m+1)/2}\kappa_{0}^{m+1}\log(\overline{d})\cdot(\overline{d}r^{*}\underline{r}\overline{r}r_{m-1})^{1/2}+C_{m}\nu\kappa_{0}^{4}\log(\overline{d})\cdot\overline{d}\underline{r}\overline{r}r_{m-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+C_{m}\nu^{m}\kappa_{0}^{2m+2}\log(\overline{d})\cdot r^{*}\underline{r}\overline{r}r_{m-1},

we have

\displaystyle\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})^{T}(({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}-(\frac{d^{*}}{n_{2m-1}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega_{2m-1}}({\mathcal{T}}^{*}))^{\langle m-1\rangle})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{Cm\kappa_{0}\sqrt{\overline{r}}}.

(38)

From (
36
) - (
A.2
) and we conclude with probability exceeding
1-m\overline{d}^{-m}
,

\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\widehat{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{Cm\kappa_{0}\sqrt{\overline{r}}}.

Finally, together with Lemma
29
, we conclude that the output
{\mathcal{T}}_{0}
satisfies

\|{\mathcal{T}}_{0}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{Cm\kappa_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\overline{r}}}\text{~and~}\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}_{0})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu.

And this finishes the proof of the lemma.

A.3
Proof of Lemma
7

Lemma 16

(Restate of Lemma
7
)
.

Suppose the conditions of
{\mathcal{T}}^{\ast}
from Theorem
4
hold and
\{\xi_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n}
are i.i.d.
\sigma_{s}
subgaussian random variables with variance
\text{Var~}\xi^{2}\leq C_{1}\sigma_{s}^{2}
for some absolute constant
C_{1}>0
. Suppose the sample size

\displaystyle n

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{2m-1}n_{i}\geq C_{m}\nu^{m+3}\kappa_{0}^{4m-4}\log^{2}(\overline{d})\bigg((d^{\ast})^{1/2}(r^{*}\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2})^{1/2}+\overline{d}r^{*}\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{m-2}\big((d_{k}\cdots d_{m})^{1/2}(r_{1}\cdots r_{k-1})^{3}r_{k}(r_{k+1}\cdots r_{m-1})^{1/2}(\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2})^{1/2}+\overline{d}(r_{1}\cdots r_{k-1})^{2}r^{*}\overline{r}\underline{r}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+(\overline{d}r^{*}\underline{r}\overline{r}r_{m-1})^{1/2}+\overline{d}\overline{r}\underline{r}r_{m-1}+r^{*}\underline{r}\overline{r}r_{m-1}\bigg),

where
C_{m}>0
depends only on
m
. Also we assume the signal-to-noise ratio satisfies

\displaystyle\underline{\sigma}/\sigma_{s}\geq C_{m}\max\{\kappa_{0}^{2m-3}(r^{*}\overline{r}\underline{r})^{1/2}(\frac{d_{1}d^{*}\log(d)}{n})^{1/2},\kappa_{0}^{m-2}(r^{*}\overline{r})^{1/4}\frac{(d^{*})^{3/4}\log^{3/2}(d)}{n^{1/2}}(1+\frac{d_{1}^{1/2}}{(d^{*})^{1/4}})\}

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+\sum_{i=2}^{m-1}\Bigg[C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{2(m-i)+5}\nu^{2}(r_{i}\cdots r_{m-1}\overline{r}\underline{r})^{1/2}\Big(\frac{(d^{*})^{1/2}d_{i}}{n}r_{i-1}\log(\overline{d})+\frac{(d^{*})^{1/2}d_{i}\cdots d_{m}}{n^{2}}r_{i-1}\log^{2}(\overline{d})

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt+\frac{(d^{*}d_{i})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{r_{i-1}}\log^{1/2}(\overline{d})\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{m-i+2}\nu(r_{i}\cdots r_{m-1}\overline{r}\underline{r})^{1/4}\Big(\frac{(d^{*}d_{i})^{1/2}}{n^{1/2}}r_{i-1}^{1/2}\log^{5/4}(\overline{d})+\frac{d_{i}^{1/2}(d_{i+1}\cdots d_{m})^{1/4}}{n^{1/2}}r_{i-1}^{3/4}\log^{5/4}(\overline{d})\Big)\Bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+C_{m}\kappa_{0}\sqrt{\overline{r}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{d^{*}d_{m}r_{m-1}}{n}\log^{1/2}(\overline{d})}+\frac{d^{*}}{n}\sqrt{\frac{r_{m-1}}{d_{1}\cdots d_{m-1}}}\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu\log(\overline{d})\Big),

Then with probability at least
1-10m\overline{d}^{-m}
, the output of Algorithm
3
satisfies

\|{\mathcal{T}}_{0}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{Cm\kappa_{0}^{2}\overline{r}^{1/2}}\quad\text{~and~}\quad\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}_{0})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu.

The proof is similar to that of Lemma
6
. We only need to modify the bound of
\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}(N_{i}-N_{i}^{*})(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)\|
. To this end, we need a noisy version of Lemma
34
and Lemma
35
. For
\|N_{1}-N_{1}^{*}\|
, we use the Theorem 2 from
Xia et al. 2021
.

Lemma 17

(
Xia et al. 2021
, Theorem 2)
.

There exists absolute constant
C_{1},C_{2}
such that for any
\alpha\geq 1
, if

n=n_{1}=n_{2}\geq C_{1}\alpha(\sqrt{d^{*}}\log(\overline{d})+\overline{d}\log^{2}(\overline{d})),

then with probability exceeding
1-\overline{d}^{-\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\|N_{1}-N_{1}^{*}\|\leq C_{2}\Big((\sigma_{s}+\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\infty})\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\sqrt{\frac{\alpha md_{1}d^{*}\log(\overline{d})}{n}}

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt+\alpha^{3}(\sigma_{s}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\infty}^{2}\log^{2}(\overline{d}))\frac{(md^{*})^{3/2}\log^{3}(\overline{d})}{n}(1+\frac{d_{1}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}})\Big).

Using this lemma and the sample size condition, under the SNR condition,

\underline{\sigma}/\sigma_{s}\geq C_{m}\max\{\kappa_{0}^{2m-3}(r^{*}\overline{r}\underline{r})^{1/2}(\frac{d_{1}d^{*}\log(d)}{n_{2}})^{1/2},\kappa_{0}^{m-2}(r^{*}\overline{r})^{1/4}\frac{(d^{*})^{3/4}\log^{3/2}(d)}{n_{2}^{1/2}}(1+\frac{d_{1}^{1/2}}{(d^{*})^{1/4}})\},

(
34
) still holds with probability exceeding
1-\overline{d}^{-m}
. For
2\leq i\leq m-1
, we need to use the following lemma.

Lemma 18
.

Let
M\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p_{1}\times p_{2}}
and
X_{i}=p_{1}p_{2}(M_{\omega_{i}}+\xi_{i})E_{\omega_{i}},Y_{j}=p_{1}p_{2}(M_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}+\xi_{j}^{\prime})E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}
, where
\omega_{i}\in\Omega_{1},\omega_{j}^{\prime}\in\Omega_{2}
are independently and uniformly sampled from
[p_{1}]\times[p_{2}]
and
|\Omega_{1}|=|\Omega_{2}|=n
with
n\leq p_{1}p_{2}
, and
\{E_{\omega}\}_{\omega\in[p_{1}]\times[p_{2}]}
is the standard basis for
{\mathbb{R}}^{p_{1}\times p_{2}}
and
\xi,\{\xi_{i}\}_{i=1}^{n},\{\xi_{j}^{\prime}\}_{j=1}^{n}
are i.i.d.
\sigma_{s}
subgaussian random variables with variance
\text{Var~}\xi^{2}\leq C_{1}\sigma_{s}^{2}
for some absolute constant
C_{1}>0
. Let
U\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p_{1}\times r}
be the orthogonal matrix such that
\textsf{Incoh}(U)\leq\sqrt{\mu}
. Then for any
\alpha\geq 1
, with probability exceeding
1-9p^{-\alpha}
where
p=\max\{p_{1},p_{2}\}
, we have for some absolute constant
C_{2}>0
,

\displaystyle\qquad\|\frac{1}{2n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}(U^{T}X_{i}Y_{j}^{T}U+U^{T}Y_{j}X_{i}^{T}U)-U^{T}MM^{T}U\|

\displaystyle\leq C_{\alpha}\|M\|_{\infty}\sigma_{s}\left(\mu r\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\log(p)+\mu r\frac{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}}\log^{2}(p)+\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\mu r\log(p)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\alpha}\sigma_{s}^{2}\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\left(\mu r\log^{3/2}(p)+\mu r\log^{5/2}(p)\frac{\sqrt{p_{2}}\sqrt{p_{2}\vee r}}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{\alpha}\log^{2}(p)\frac{p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}}{n}\left(\mu rp_{2}^{1/2}+\frac{\mu rp_{2}}{n}+(\frac{\mu rn}{\log^{3}(p)})^{1/2}\right).

We can use this lemma to bound
\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}(N_{i}-N_{i}^{*})(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)\|
. Using the same notations as in the proof of Lemma
6
, we denote
b_{i-1}=2\sqrt{r_{i}}\underline{\sigma}^{-2}\|(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}(N_{i}-N_{i}^{*})(\widehat{T}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)\|
and
q_{i-1}=80\sqrt{r_{i}}\kappa_{0}^{2}
.
For any
2\leq i\leq m-1
, under the sample size condition and the SNR condition,

\displaystyle\underline{\sigma}/\sigma_{s}\geq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{2(m-i)+5}\nu^{2}(r_{i}\cdots r_{m-1}\overline{r}\underline{r})^{1/2}\Big(\frac{(d^{*})^{1/2}d_{i}}{n_{2i}}r_{i-1}\log(\overline{d})+\frac{(d^{*})^{1/2}d_{i}\cdots d_{m}}{n_{2i}^{2}}r_{i-1}\log^{2}(\overline{d})

\displaystyle\hskip 199.16928pt+\frac{(d^{*}d_{i})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{n_{2i}}}\sqrt{r_{i-1}}\log^{1/2}(\overline{d})\Big)

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{m-i+2}\nu(r_{i}\cdots r_{m-1}\overline{r}\underline{r})^{1/4}\Big(\frac{(d^{*}d_{i})^{1/2}}{n_{2i}^{1/2}}r_{i-1}^{1/2}\log^{5/4}(\overline{d})+\frac{d_{i}^{1/2}(d_{i+1}\cdots d_{m})^{1/4}}{n_{2i}^{1/2}}r_{i-1}^{3/4}\log^{5/4}(\overline{d})\Big),

with probability exceeding
1-9\overline{d}^{-m}
, we have
q_{m-2}\cdots q_{i}b_{i-1}\leq\frac{1}{Cm^{2}\kappa_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\overline{r}}}
holds. And we obtain (
36
), that is,

d_{c}(\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1},T^{*\leq m-1})\leq(Cm\kappa_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\overline{r}})^{-1}.

We still need to bound
n_{2m-1}^{-1}d^{*}\|T^{\leq m-1\top}\sum_{i\in\Omega_{2m-1}}\xi_{i}{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega_{i}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}
. Notice this is already bounded in (
48
). We get with probability exceeding
1-\overline{d}^{-m}
,

\displaystyle\|T^{\leq m-1\top}\sum_{i\in\Omega_{2m-1}}\xi_{i}{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega_{i}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|\leq C_{m}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{d^{*}d_{m}}{n_{2m-1}}}\log^{1/2}(\overline{d})+\frac{d^{*}}{n_{2m-1}}\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu\sqrt{\frac{r_{m-1}}{d_{1}\cdots d_{m-1}}}\log(\overline{d})\Big)\sigma_{s}.

Therefore under the SNR condition,

\displaystyle\underline{\sigma}/\sigma_{s}\geq C_{m}\kappa_{0}\sqrt{\overline{r}}\Big(\sqrt{\frac{d^{*}d_{m}r_{m-1}}{n_{2m-1}}\log^{1/2}(\overline{d})}+\frac{d^{*}}{n}\sqrt{\frac{r_{m-1}}{d_{1}\cdots d_{m-1}}}\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu\log(\overline{d})\Big),

we get (
38
) holds with probability exceeding
1-\overline{d}^{-m}
. Put all these together and we get with probability exceeding
1-(9(m-2)+2)\overline{d}^{-m}
,

\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\widehat{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{Cm\kappa_{0}\sqrt{\overline{r}}}.

Finally, together with Lemma
29
, we conclude that the output
{\mathcal{T}}_{0}
satisfies

\|{\mathcal{T}}_{0}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{Cm\kappa_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\overline{r}}}\text{~and~}\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}_{0})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu.

And this finishes the proof of the lemma.

A.4
Proof of Lemma
8

For notion simplicity, we denote
{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{j}{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega_{j}}
.
We only need to slightly modify the proof of Lemma
5
in Section
A.1.4
. Notice now we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l+1}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}(\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}_{l})-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\overset{\text{Lemma}~\ref{lemma:tt-perturbation}}{\leq}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{600m\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\underline{\sigma}},

(39)

where Lemma
26
is valid only when
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq c_{m}\underline{\sigma}
for some sufficient
c_{m}>0
depending only on
m
, which we will verify momentarily.
From the way we choose
\zeta_{l}
, we have
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
. Now we estimate
\|{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\alpha_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})+\alpha_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\delta)\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\alpha_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+(1+\delta^{-1})\alpha_{l}^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(40)

Now we need to bound
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}
.
In the following we shall drop the subscript of
{\mathcal{T}}_{l}
for simplicity. We have

\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2\|({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}){\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. And

\displaystyle\|({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}){\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}=\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}}),({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})({\mathcal{X}}_{0})\rangle\leq\|{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|\cdot\|({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})({\mathcal{X}}_{0})\|_{*}

for some
{\mathcal{X}}_{0}
with
\|{\mathcal{X}}_{0}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 1
. Notice
({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})({\mathcal{X}}_{0})
has TT-rank at most
4(r_{1},\cdots,r_{m-1})
from Lemma
24
. Applying Lemma
25
and we obtain

\displaystyle\|({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})({\mathcal{X}}_{0})\|_{*}\leq 2^{m-1}\sqrt{r^{*}}\|({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})({\mathcal{X}}_{0})\|_{\rm{F}}.

Denote
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{X}}_{0})=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}
and
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{X}}_{0})=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}^{*}
with

\displaystyle\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}^{\langle i\rangle}=(T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)(I-L(T_{i})L(T_{i})^{T})(T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}V_{i+1}^{T},

\displaystyle\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}^{*\langle i\rangle}=(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}.

In the following for an orthogonal matrix
U
, we denote
P_{U}=UU^{T}
.
Now for
1\leq i\leq m
, we consider
\|\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
. Notice

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636ptP_{T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}P_{V_{i+1}}-P_{T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}P_{V_{i+1}^{*}}

\displaystyle=(P_{T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I}-P_{T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I}){\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}P_{V_{i+1}}+P_{T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}(P_{V_{i+1}}-P_{V_{i+1}^{*}}).

Since
T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I
is the top
d_{i}r_{i-1}
left singular vectors of
{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}
and
T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I
is the top
d_{i}r_{i-1}
left singular vectors of
{\mathcal{T}}^{*\langle i\rangle}
, we have

\displaystyle\|P_{T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I}-P_{T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I}\|\leq\frac{2\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\sigma}},\quad\|P_{V_{i+1}}-P_{V_{i+1}^{*}}\|\leq\frac{2\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\sigma}}.

This implies

\displaystyle\|(P_{T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I}-P_{T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I}){\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}P_{V_{i+1}}+P_{T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}(P_{V_{i+1}}-P_{V_{i+1}^{*}})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{16\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}.

And similarly we can bound

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\Big\|(T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)L(T_{i})L(T_{i})^{T}(T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}V_{i+1}^{T}

\displaystyle\hskip 113.81102pt-(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T}(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}\Big\|_{\rm F}

\displaystyle=\|P_{T^{\leq i}}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}P_{V_{i+1}}-P_{T^{*\leq i}}{\mathcal{X}}_{0}^{\langle i\rangle}P_{V_{i+1}^{*}}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}.

Therefore

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{X}}_{0})-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{X}}_{0})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|\sum_{i=1}^{m}\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq m\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}-\delta{\mathcal{X}}_{i}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 32m^{2}\frac{\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}.

(41)

On the other hand, it is proved in (Theorem 1,
Xia et al. 2021
) that with probability exceeding
1-\overline{d}^{-m}
,

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|\leq C_{m}\sigma_{s}(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d}).

(42)

According to (
41
) and (
42
), we get

\displaystyle\|({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}){\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq C_{m}\frac{\sigma_{s}}{\underline{\sigma}}(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1)\sqrt{r^{*}}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Now we bound
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}
.
From the definition of
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}
in
Section
4.3
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|\delta{\mathcal{A}}_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\ldots+\|\delta{\mathcal{A}}_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

such that for
i\in[m-1]
,

\displaystyle\|\delta{\mathcal{A}}_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}(V_{i+1}^{*})^{T}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(T^{*\leq i}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

and
\|\delta{\mathcal{A}}_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|(T^{*\leq m-1})^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.
Recall from induction we assume
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu=:\sqrt{\mu}
. Now we shall use Bernstein’s inequality to bound
\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}\|
. We denote
{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})^{\langle i\rangle}=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{j}E_{j}
, where
E_{j}={\mathcal{E}}_{\omega_{j}}^{\langle i\rangle}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}E_{j}V_{i+1}^{*}\|\leq\|V_{i+1}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I\|_{2,\infty}=\|V_{i+1}^{*}\|_{2,\infty}\|T^{*\leq i-1}\|_{2,\infty}\leq\sqrt{\frac{r_{i-1}d_{i}r_{i}}{d^{*}}}\mu,

where the last inequality holds since
\textsf{Incoh}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\leq\sqrt{\mu}
. And thus for all
j\in[n]
, we have

\displaystyle\big\|\|\xi_{j}(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}E_{j}V_{i+1}^{*}\|\big\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq\sigma_{s}\sqrt{\frac{r_{i-1}d_{i}r_{i}}{d^{*}}}\mu.

On the other hand, we have

\|{\mathbb{E}}\xi_{j}^{2}(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}E_{j}V_{i+1}^{*}(V_{i+1}^{*})^{T}E_{j}^{T}(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)\|\leq\sigma_{s}^{2}\frac{r_{i-1}d_{i}r_{i}}{d^{*}}\mu^{2}

and

\|{\mathbb{E}}\xi_{j}^{2}(V_{i+1}^{*})^{T}E_{j}^{T}(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}E_{j}V_{i+1}^{*}\|\leq\sigma_{s}^{2}\frac{r_{i-1}d_{i}r_{i}}{d^{*}}\mu^{2}.

Now from Theorem
30
, we have with probability exceeding
1-\overline{d}^{-m}
,

\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}\|\leq C_{m}\mu\sigma_{s}\sqrt{\frac{nr_{i-1}d_{i}r_{i}}{d^{*}}\log(\overline{d})},

where
C_{m}>0
is some absolute constant depending only on
m
.
Therefore we have

\|\delta{\mathcal{A}}_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{8}\nu^{4}\sigma_{s}^{2}\frac{nr_{i-1}d_{i}r_{i}^{2}}{d^{*}}\log(\overline{d}).

Similarly, we have
\|\delta{\mathcal{A}}_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{4}\nu^{2}\sigma_{s}^{2}\frac{nr_{m-1}^{2}d_{m}}{d^{*}}\log(\overline{d})
. Combine these bounds and we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{8}\nu^{4}\sigma_{s}^{2}\frac{nr_{i-1}d_{i}r_{i}^{2}}{d^{*}}\log(\overline{d})+C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{4}\nu^{2}\sigma_{s}^{2}\frac{nr_{m-1}^{2}d_{m}}{d^{*}}\log(\overline{d})

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{8}\nu^{4}\frac{n}{d^{*}}\sigma_{s}^{2}\overline{r}\cdot\textsf{dof},

where
\textsf{dof}=\sum_{i=1}^{m}r_{i-1}d_{i}r_{i}
is the degree of freedom of a TT-rank
\boldsymbol{r}
tensor.

\displaystyle\alpha_{l}^{2}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{S}})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{m}\frac{\sigma_{s}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}(\frac{d^{*}\overline{d}}{n}+\frac{(d^{*})^{2}}{n^{2}})r^{*}\log^{2m+4}(\overline{d})\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{8}\nu^{4}\frac{d^{*}}{n}\sigma_{s}^{2}\overline{r}\cdot\textsf{dof}.

(43)

Under the SNR condition,

\underline{\sigma}/\sigma_{s}\geq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{d^{*}\overline{d}}{n}}+\frac{d^{*}}{n}\right)r^{*}\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})
,
we go back to (
A.4
), and we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{W}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(1+\delta)\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\alpha_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C_{m}(1+\delta^{-1})\kappa_{0}^{8}\nu^{4}\frac{d^{*}}{n}\sigma_{s}^{2}\overline{r}\cdot\textsf{dof}\cdot\log(\overline{d})

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt+10^{-6}\cdot(1+\delta^{-1})\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(44)

Since we already bound
\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-\alpha_{l}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}_{l}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
in Section
A.1.4
. Now we choose
\delta=0.001
, together with (
39
), (
A.4
) and under the SNR condition, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l+1}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 0.98\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{8}\nu^{4}\frac{d^{*}}{n}\sigma_{s}^{2}\overline{r}\cdot\textsf{dof}\cdot\log(\overline{d}).

After at most
l_{\max}=\Omega\left(\log(C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{10}\nu^{4}\overline{r}^{2}\frac{d^{*}}{n}(\frac{\sigma_{s}}{\underline{\sigma}})^{2}\cdot\textsf{dof})\right)
iterations, we have

\|{\mathcal{T}}_{l_{\max}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq C_{m}\kappa_{0}^{8}\nu^{4}\frac{d^{*}}{n}\sigma_{s}^{2}\overline{r}\cdot\textsf{dof}\cdot\log(\overline{d}).

Appendix B
Technical Lemmas

In this section, we provide some technical lemmas. Some proofs for the lemmas are placed to the next section.

Theorem 19

(Wedin’s sin
\Theta
theorem)
.

Let
M^{*}
,
M=M^{*}+E\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p_{1}\times p_{2}}
be two matrices. Let
U^{*},U
be the top
r
left singular vectors of
M^{*},M
respectively. If
\|E\|<\sigma_{r}^{*}-\sigma_{r+1}^{*}
, then

\min_{R\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r}}\|UR-R^{*}\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\|E\|}{\sigma_{r}^{*}-\sigma_{r+1}^{*}-\|E\|},

where
\sigma_{i}^{*}
is the
i
-th largest singular value of
M^{*}
, and
{\mathbb{O}}_{r}
is the set of
r\times r
orthogonal matrices.

B.1
Lemmas about linear algebra

Lemma 20
.

Let
N\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{N}\times d_{1}\cdots d_{i}}
and
M\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{i+2}\ldots d_{m}\times d_{M}}
, then we have the following relations:

\displaystyle\textsf{reshape}(N{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle},[d_{N}d_{i+1},d_{i+2}\cdots d_{m}])=(N\otimes I_{d_{i+1}}){\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i+1\rangle},

\displaystyle\textsf{reshape}\left((N\otimes I_{d_{i+1}}){\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i+1\rangle},[d_{N},d_{i+1}\cdots d_{m}]\right)=N{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle},

\displaystyle\textsf{reshape}({\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i+1\rangle}M,[d_{1}\ldots d_{i},d_{i+1}d_{M}])={\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}(I_{d_{i+1}}\otimes M),

\displaystyle\textsf{reshape}({\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}(I_{d_{i+1}}\otimes M),[d_{1}\ldots d_{i+1},d_{M}])={\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i+1\rangle}M.

Proof.

We first show the first equation. For all
x_{N}\in[d_{N}]
and
x_{j}\in[d_{j}]
, we have

\displaystyle N{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}(x_{N};x_{i+1}\cdots x_{m})

\displaystyle=\sum_{x_{1},\cdots,x_{i}}N(x_{N};x_{1},\cdots,x_{i}){\mathcal{T}}(x_{1},\cdots,x_{m})

\displaystyle=\sum_{x_{1},\cdots,x_{i},x_{i+1}^{\prime}}N(x_{N};x_{1},\cdots,x_{i})I(x_{i+1},x_{i+1}^{\prime}){\mathcal{T}}(x_{1},\cdots,x_{i+1}^{\prime},\cdots,x_{m})

\displaystyle=\sum_{x_{1},\cdots,x_{i},x_{i+1}^{\prime}}(N\otimes I_{d_{i+1}})(x_{N},x_{i+1};x_{1},\cdots,x_{i},x_{i+1}^{\prime}){\mathcal{T}}(x_{1},\cdots,x_{i+1}^{\prime},\cdots,x_{m}).

So from this we get the desired result. Now the second equation is just the inverse statement of the first one. And the third and fourth equations are similar to the first one.
∎

Lemma 21
.

For any matrix
A\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times m},x\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}
, we have

\|A^{T}x\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\sqrt{n}\|A\|_{2,\infty}\|x\|_{\ell_{2}}.

Let another matrix
B\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times m}
, then we have

\|AB^{T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\sqrt{m}\|A\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\|B\|_{2,\infty}.

When we have another matrix
X\in{\mathbb{R}}_{m\times m}
and
m\leq p,m\leq n
, we have

\|AXB^{T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\|X\|\cdot\|A\|_{2,\infty}\|B\|_{2,\infty}.

Proof.

Set
A=\begin{bmatrix}a_{1}^{T}\\
\vdots\\
a_{n}^{T}\\
\end{bmatrix}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times m}
, then
A^{T}x=\sum_{i=1}^{n}x_{i}a_{i}
. And from Cauchy-Schwartz inequality, we have

\|A^{T}x\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\leq\|x\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|a_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\leq n\|x\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}\|A\|_{2,\infty}^{2}.

Also set
B=\begin{bmatrix}b_{1}^{T}\\
\vdots\\
b_{p}^{T}\\
\end{bmatrix}
, then

\|AB^{T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}=\max_{j,k}|a_{j}^{T}b_{k}|\leq\max_{j,k}\|a_{j}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\|b_{k}\|_{\ell_{1}}\leq\sqrt{m}\|A\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\max_{k}\|b_{k}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\sqrt{m}\|A\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\cdot\|B\|_{2,\infty}.

Finally, let
X=U\Sigma V^{T}
be its SVD,

\displaystyle\|AXB^{T}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|AX\|_{2,\infty}\|B\|_{2,\infty}=\|AU\Sigma\|_{2,\infty}\|B\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|X\|\|AU\|_{2,\infty}\|B\|_{2,\infty}=\|X\|\|A\|_{2,\infty}\|B\|_{2,\infty}.

∎

Lemma 22
.

Let
A_{1},A_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
be two rank
r
matrices with the decomposition
A_{1}=U_{1}\Sigma_{1}V_{1}^{T},A_{2}=U_{2}\Sigma_{2}V_{2}^{T}
such that
U_{1}^{T}U_{1}=U_{2}^{T}U_{2}=V_{1}^{T}V_{1}=V_{2}^{T}V_{2}=I_{r}
and
\Sigma_{1},\Sigma_{2}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r\times r}
be invertible and
U_{1},U_{2}
,
V_{1},V_{2}
are well aligned in the sense that
d_{c}(U_{1},U_{2})=\|U_{1}-U_{2}\|_{\rm{F}}
and
d_{c}(V_{1},V_{2})=\|V_{1}-V_{2}\|_{\rm{F}}
.
Suppose that
\|A_{1}-A_{2}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{10}\min\{\sigma_{\min}(A_{1}),\sigma_{\min}(A_{2})\}
, then we have

\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 4\sqrt{r}\kappa\|A_{1}-A_{2}\|,

where
\kappa=\max\{\kappa(A_{1}),\kappa(A_{2})\}
and
\sigma_{\min}(A)
is the smallest nonzero singular value of
A
.

Proof.

We can decompose
\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}
as follows,

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{1}-\Sigma_{2}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\|(U_{1}-U_{2})^{T}A_{1}V_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|U_{2}^{T}(A_{1}-A_{2})V_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|U_{2}^{T}A_{2}(V_{1}-V_{2})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq(\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{2r}\kappa(A_{1})+\sqrt{2r}\kappa(A_{2}))\|A_{1}-A_{2}\|\leq 4\sqrt{r}\kappa\|A_{1}-A_{2}\|,

where the first inequality in the second line follows Wedin’s sin
\Theta
theorem.
∎

Lemma 23
.

Let
A\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
and
B\in{\mathbb{R}}^{np\times q}
be such that
A^{T}A=I_{n},B^{T}B=I_{q}
and
\textsf{Incoh}(A)\leq\sqrt{\mu_{1}},\textsf{Incoh}(B)\leq\sqrt{\mu_{2}}
. Then we have
\textsf{Incoh}((A\otimes I_{p})B)\leq\sqrt{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}n}
.

Proof.

Consider for any
k\in[mp]
,
\|e_{k}^{T}(A\otimes I_{p})B\|_{\ell_{2}}
, denote by
a_{k}^{T}
the
k
-th row of
A\otimes I_{p}
, and thus
\|a_{k}\|_{\ell_{0}}\leq n
. So we have

\|e_{k}^{T}(A\otimes I_{p})B\|_{\ell_{2}}=\|a_{k}^{T}B\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\mu_{1}n}{m}}\sqrt{n\frac{\mu_{2}q}{np}}=\sqrt{\mu_{1}\mu_{2}n\frac{q}{mp}}.

∎

B.2
Lemmas concerning TT format

Lemma 24

(Facts about TT rank)
.

1.

Let
{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{B}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\ldots\times d_{m}}
be two tensors satisfies
\textsf{rank}_{\textsf{tt}}({\mathcal{A}})=(r_{1},\ldots,r_{m-1})
and
\textsf{rank}_{\textsf{tt}}({\mathcal{B}})=(s_{1},\ldots,s_{m-1})
. Then we have

\textsf{rank}_{\textsf{tt}}({\mathcal{A}}+{\mathcal{B}})\leq(r_{1}+s_{1},\ldots,r_{m-1}+s_{m-1}).

2.

Let
{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\in{{\mathbb{M}}}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{\textsf{tt}}
and
{\mathbb{T}}^{*}
be the corresponding tangent space. Let
{\mathcal{T}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\ldots\times d_{m}}
be an arbitrary tensor. Then the rank of
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}})
satisfies
\textsf{rank}_{\textsf{tt}}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}}))\leq(2r_{1},\ldots,2r_{m-1})
.

Proof.

1.

It follows from
\textsf{rank}(({\mathcal{A}}+{\mathcal{B}})^{\langle i\rangle})\leq\textsf{rank}({\mathcal{A}}^{\langle i\rangle})+\textsf{rank}({\mathcal{B}}^{\langle i\rangle})=r_{i}+s_{i}
.

2.

Since
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}})=\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{1}+\ldots+\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{m}
, where
\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{i}=[T_{1}^{*},\ldots,T_{i-1}^{*},X_{i},T_{i+1}^{*},\ldots,T_{m}^{*}]
and the expression of
X_{i}
are give in (
4.3
). Now we consider the
i-th
separation rank of
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}})
. Notice that for all
j\leq i
, we have
\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{j}^{\langle i\rangle}=T^{*\leq i}_{j}T^{*\geq i+1}
, where
T^{*\leq i}_{j}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\ldots d_{i}\times r_{i}}
is the matrix generated by
T_{1}^{*},\ldots,X_{j},\ldots,T_{i}^{*}
. And for
j\geq i+1
, we have
\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{j}^{\langle i\rangle}=T^{*\leq i}T^{*\geq i+1}_{j}
, where
T^{*\geq i}_{j}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{i}\times d_{i+1}\ldots d_{m}}
is the matrix generated by
T_{i+1}^{*},\ldots,X_{j},\ldots,T_{m}^{*}
. So we have

{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}})^{\langle i\rangle}=(\sum_{j=1}^{i}T^{*\leq i}_{j})T^{*\geq i+1}+T^{*\leq i}(\sum_{j=i+1}^{m}T^{*\geq i+1}_{j}).

And thus
\textsf{rank}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{T}})^{\langle i\rangle})\leq 2r_{i}
.

∎

Lemma 25
.

Let
{\mathcal{T}}\in{{\mathbb{M}}}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{\textsf{tt}}
be a tensor of TT rank
(r_{1},\ldots,r_{m-1})
with a left orthogonal decomposition
{\mathcal{T}}=[T_{1},\ldots,T_{m}]
. Then we have

\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{*}\leq\sqrt{r_{1}\ldots r_{m-1}}\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Proof.

Using the alternative definition for the tensor nuclear norm from in
Friedland and Lim 2014
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{*}=\min\{\sum_{i=1}^{s}|\lambda_{i}|:{\mathcal{T}}=\sum_{i=1}^{s}\lambda_{i}u_{1,i}\otimes\ldots\otimes u_{m,i},\|u_{l,i}\|_{\ell_{2}}=1,l\in[m],i\in[s],s\in{\mathbb{N}}\}.

(45)

So we can write
{\mathcal{T}}
as sum of rank one tensors in the following form

{\mathcal{T}}=\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r_{1}}\cdots\sum_{k_{m-1}=1}^{r_{m-1}}T_{1}(\cdot,k_{1})\otimes T_{2}(k_{1},\cdot,k_{2})\otimes\ldots\otimes T_{m}(k_{m-1},\cdot),

where each
T_{i}(k_{i-1},\cdot,k_{i})\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{i}}
is a vector. For each fixed
k_{1},\ldots,k_{m-1}
, we have

\|T_{1}(\cdot,k_{1})\otimes T_{2}(k_{1},\cdot,k_{2})\otimes\ldots\otimes T_{m}(k_{m-1},\cdot)\|_{\rm{F}}=\prod_{i=1}^{m}\|T_{i}(k_{i-1},\cdot,k_{i})\|_{\ell_{2}}.

And

\displaystyle\big(\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r_{1}}\cdots\sum_{k_{m-1}=1}^{r_{m-1}}\prod_{i=1}^{m}

\displaystyle\|T_{i}(k_{i-1},\cdot,k_{i})\|_{\ell_{2}}\big)^{2}\leq r_{1}\ldots r_{m-1}\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r_{1}}\cdots\sum_{k_{m-1}=1}^{r_{m-1}}\prod_{i=1}^{m}\|T_{i}(k_{i-1},\cdot,k_{i})\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}r_{1}\ldots r_{m-1}\cdot\sum_{k_{1},\ldots,k_{m-1}}\prod_{i=2}^{m}\|T_{i}(k_{i-1},\cdot,k_{i})\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\ldots

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}r_{1}\ldots r_{m-1}\cdot\sum_{k_{m-1}}\|T_{m}(k_{m-1},\cdot)\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}=r_{1}\ldots r_{m-1}\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where
(a)
holds since we have
\|T_{1}(\cdot,k_{1})\|_{\ell_{2}}=1
since
L(T_{1})L(T_{1})^{T}=I
and
(b)
holds for similar reason and we use
L(T_{i})^{T}L(T_{i})=I_{r_{i}}
for all
i\in[m-1]
.
Together with
\|T_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}
and (
45
), we finish the proof.
∎

Lemma 26

(Perturbation bound for TT SVD)
.

Let
{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\in{{\mathbb{M}}}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{\textsf{tt}}
be the m-way tensor and
\underline{\sigma}:=\min_{i=1}^{m-1}\sigma_{\min}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}
. And we denote the tensor
{\mathcal{T}}={\mathcal{T}}^{*}+{\mathcal{D}}
.
Then suppose
\underline{\sigma}\geq C_{m}\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}
for some constant
C_{m}\geq 500m
depending only on
m
, we have

\|\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}({\mathcal{T}})-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{600m\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\underline{\sigma}}.

Proof.

See Section
C.7
.
∎

Lemma 27
.

Let
{\mathcal{T}},{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\in{{\mathbb{M}}}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{\textsf{tt}}
be two TT-rank
{\boldsymbol{r}}
tensors. Suppose we have
8\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\underline{\sigma}
, then we have

\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{12\sqrt{2}m\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}},

where
{\mathbb{T}}
is the tangent space at the point
{\mathcal{T}}
.

Proof.

See Section
C.8
.
∎

Interchanging the roles of
{\mathcal{T}}
and
{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
in the theorem and using Weyl’s inequality and we get the following corollary.

Corollary 28
.

Under the setting of Lemma
27
, let
{\mathbb{T}}^{*}
be the corresponding tangent space at
{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
, then we have

\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}})\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{20m\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}}.

Lemma 29

(TTSVD + Trim implies incoherence)
.

Let
{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\in{{\mathbb{M}}}_{\boldsymbol{r}}^{\textsf{tt}}
be such that
\textsf{Spiki}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\leq\nu
. Suppose that
{\mathcal{W}}
satisfies
\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{600m\sqrt{\overline{r}}\kappa_{0}}
, then we have
\textsf{Incoh}\left(\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}(\textsf{Trim}_{\zeta}({\mathcal{W}}))\right)\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu
if we choose
\zeta=\frac{10\|{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm{F}}}{9\sqrt{d^{*}}}\nu
. Furthermore,

\|\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}(\textsf{Trim}_{\zeta}({\mathcal{W}}))-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{2}\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}.

Proof.

See Section
C.9
.
∎

B.3
Concentration inequalities

Define the upper bound of the
\psi_{\alpha}
norm of
\|Z\|
as

U^{(\alpha)}:=\inf\{u>0:{\mathbb{E}}\exp(\|Z\|^{\alpha}/u^{\alpha})\leq 2\},\quad\alpha\geq 1

and

\sigma^{2}=\max\left\{\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}Z_{i}Z_{i}^{T}\|,\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathbb{E}}Z_{i}^{T}Z_{i}\|\right\}.

Theorem 30

(
Koltchinskii et al. 2011
)
.

Let
Z,Z_{1},\ldots,Z_{n}
be i.i.d. random matrices with dimensions
d_{1}\times d_{2}
that satisfy
{\mathbb{E}}Z=0
and
U^{(\alpha)}<\infty
for some
\alpha\geq 1
. Then there exists some absolute constant
C>0
such that for all
t>0
, with probability at least
1-e^{-t}
, we have

\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}\|\leq C\max\left\{\sigma\sqrt{\frac{t+\log d}{n}},\quad U^{(\alpha)}\left(\log\frac{U^{(\alpha)}}{\sigma}\right)^{1/\alpha}\cdot\frac{t+\log d}{n}\right\},

where
d=d_{1}+d_{2}
.

First we introduce some operators for the following lemma. For all
x\in[d_{1}]\times\ldots\times[d_{m}]
, let the operator
{\mathcal{P}}_{x}:{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\ldots\times d_{m}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\ldots\times d_{m}}
be defined by
{\mathcal{P}}_{x}({\mathcal{X}})=\langle{\mathcal{X}},{\mathcal{E}}_{x}\rangle{\mathcal{E}}_{x}
. And let
\Omega=\{\omega_{1},\ldots,\omega_{n}\}
be the sampling set and define
{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}:{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\ldots\times d_{m}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\ldots\times d_{m}}
by

{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}({\mathcal{X}})=\sum_{i=1}^{m}\langle{\mathcal{X}},{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega_{i}}\rangle{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega_{i}}
.

Lemma 31

(Concentration inequality)
.

Suppose
\Omega
with
|\Omega|=n
is a set of indices sampled independently and uniformly with replacement. Suppose
\textsf{Spiki}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\leq\nu
for some
\nu>0
. When

n\geq C_{m}(\nu\kappa_{0})^{4}\bar{d}\bar{r}^{2}\log(d^{*})

for some large constant
C_{1}>0
, with probability exceeding
1-(\overline{d})^{-m}
, we have

\|\frac{d^{*}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\|\leq\frac{1}{2}.

Proof.

First we define the operators:

{\mathcal{Z}}_{x}:=\frac{d^{*}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}-\frac{1}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}},\quad x\in[d_{1}]\times\ldots\times[d_{m}].

Then
\frac{d^{*}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathcal{Z}}_{\omega_{i}}
. We first estimate an upper bound for
\|{\mathcal{Z}}_{\omega_{i}}\|
. First notice

({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})^{2}({\mathcal{X}})=\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{E}}_{x})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})({\mathcal{X}}).

This implies
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\|\leq\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{E}}_{x})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. And

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{Z}}_{x}\|\leq\frac{d^{*}}{n}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\|+\frac{1}{n}\leq\frac{d^{*}}{n}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{E}}_{x})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{n}\leq\frac{2m(\nu\kappa_{0})^{4}\bar{d}\bar{r}^{2}}{n},

where the last inequality we use
\max_{x}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{E}}_{x})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{m(\nu\kappa_{0})^{4}\bar{d}\bar{r}^{2}}{n}
, which comes from Lemma
2
. Now we bound
\|{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathcal{Z}}_{\omega_{i}}^{2}\|
. Simple calculations show that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathcal{Z}}_{\omega_{i}}^{2}=\frac{d^{*}}{n}\sum_{x\in[d_{1}]\times\ldots\times[d_{m}]}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})^{2}-\frac{1}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}.

And this implies

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\mathcal{Z}}_{\omega_{i}}^{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{d^{*}}{n}\|\sum_{x}({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})^{2}\|+\frac{1}{n}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{d^{*}}{n}\max_{x}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{E}}_{x})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|\sum_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\|+\frac{1}{n}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2m(\nu\kappa_{0})^{4}\bar{d}\bar{r}^{2}}{n},

where in
(a)
we use the fact that
({\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}})^{2}\leq\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}({\mathcal{E}}_{x})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{x}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}
, where
{\mathcal{A}}\leq{\mathcal{B}}
means
{\mathcal{B}}-{\mathcal{A}}
is an SPD operator. So we conclude using operator Bernstein inequality,

\|\frac{d^{*}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}-{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}^{*}}\|\leq C\left(\sqrt{\frac{m(\nu\kappa_{0})^{4}\bar{d}\bar{r}^{2}\log(d^{*})}{n}}+\frac{m(\nu\kappa_{0})^{4}\bar{d}\bar{r}^{2}\log(d^{*})}{n}\right)

with probability exceeding
1-(\overline{d})^{-m}
. When
n\geq C_{m}(\nu\kappa_{0})^{4}\bar{d}\bar{r}^{2}\log(d^{*})
, the right hand side is less than
1/2
. And this finishes the proof of the lemma.
∎

Lemma 32

(
Xia et al. 2021
, Theorem 1)
.

Suppose
\Omega
is the set sampled uniformly with replacement with size
|\Omega|=n
, then we have with probability exceeding
1-(\overline{d})^{-m}
, the following holds

\|({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}})({\mathcal{J}})\|\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d}),

where
{\mathcal{J}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d_{1}\times\cdots\times d_{m}}
is the tensor with all its entries
1
.

Lemma 33
.

Let
\Omega=\{\omega_{i}:i=1,\cdots,n\}
where
\omega_{i}
is independently and uniformly sampled from the set of collections
[d_{1}]\times\cdots\times[d_{m}]
.
With probability at least
1-\overline{d}^{-m}
, the maximum number of repetitions of any entry in
\Omega
is less than
2m\log(\overline{d})
.

Proof.

This is a result of standard Chenroff bound. See for example (
Recht 2011
, Proposition 5) for a proof.
∎

B.4
Lemmas for initialization

Lemma 34

(
Xia and Yuan 2019
, Theorem 2)
.

Let
M\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p_{1}\times p_{2}}
and
X_{i}=p_{1}p_{2}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{i}}M
,
Y_{j}=p_{1}p_{2}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}M
, where
\omega_{i}\in\Omega_{1},\omega_{j}^{\prime}\in\Omega_{2}
are independently and uniformly sampled from
[p_{1}]\times[p_{2}]
and
|\Omega_{1}|=|\Omega_{2}|=n
. Denote by
N=MM^{T}
and
\widetilde{N}=\frac{1}{2n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}(X_{i}Y_{j}^{T}+Y_{j}X_{i}^{T})
, then with probability exceeding
1-p^{-\alpha}
with
p=\max\{p_{1},p_{2}\}
, we have

\|\widetilde{N}-N\|\leq C\alpha^{2}\frac{p_{1}^{3/2}p_{2}^{3/2}\log(p)}{n}\left[\left(1+\frac{p_{1}}{p_{2}}\right)^{1/2}+\frac{p_{1}^{1/2}p_{2}^{1/2}}{n}+\left(\frac{n}{p_{2}\log(p)}\right)^{1/2}\right]\cdot\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}.

Lemma 35
.

Let
M\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p_{1}\times p_{2}}
and
