X_{i}=p_{1}p_{2}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{i}}M,Y_{j}=p_{1}p_{2}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}M
, where
\omega_{i}\in\Omega_{1},\omega_{j}^{\prime}\in\Omega_{2}
are independently and uniformly sampled from
[p_{1}]\times[p_{2}]
and
|\Omega_{1}|=|\Omega_{2}|=n
. Let
U\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p_{1}\times r}
be the orthogonal matrix such that
\textsf{Incoh}(U)\leq\sqrt{\mu}
. Then with probability exceeding
1-p^{-\alpha}
, we have

\displaystyle\qquad\|\frac{1}{2n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}(U^{T}X_{i}Y_{j}^{T}U+U^{T}Y_{j}X_{i}^{T}U)-U^{T}MM^{T}U\|

\displaystyle\leq C\alpha^{2}\log^{2}(p)\frac{p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}}{n}\left(\mu rp_{2}^{1/2}+\frac{\mu rp_{2}}{n}+(\frac{\mu rn}{\log^{3}(p)})^{1/2}\right).

Proof.

See Section
C.10
.
∎

When
\textsf{Spiki}(M)\leq\nu
, we have

\displaystyle\qquad\|\frac{1}{2n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}(U^{T}X_{i}Y_{j}^{T}U+U^{T}Y_{j}X_{i}^{T}U)-U^{T}MM^{T}U\|

\displaystyle\leq C\alpha^{2}\log^{2}(p)\nu^{2}\|M\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\left(\frac{\mu rp_{2}^{1/2}}{n}+\frac{\mu rp_{2}}{n^{2}}+(\frac{\mu r}{n\log^{3}(p)})^{1/2}\right)

holds with probability exceeding
1-p^{-\alpha}
.

Lemma 36
.

Let
M\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p_{1}\times p_{2}}
and
X_{i}=p_{1}p_{2}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{i}}(M)
, where
\omega_{i}\in\Omega
is independently and uniformly sampled in
[p_{1}]\times[p_{2}]
and
|\Omega|=n
. Let
U\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p_{1}\times r}
be the orthogonal matrix such that
\textsf{Incoh}(U)\leq\sqrt{\mu}
. Then with probability exceeding
1-p^{-\alpha}
, we have

\|U^{T}(\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)-M)\|\leq C\alpha\left(\frac{\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}\sqrt{\mu r}\|M\|_{\infty}\log(p)}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}(\mu r\vee p_{2})\log(p)}{n}}\right).

Proof.

See appendix
C.11
.
∎

Lemma 37

(
Keshavan et al. 2010
, Remark 6.2)
.

Let
U,X\in{\mathbb{R}}^{p\times r}
be orthogonal and
\textsf{Incoh}(U)\leq\sqrt{\mu_{0}}
and
d_{p}(U,X)\leq\delta\leq\frac{1}{16\pi}
. Then
\bar{X}
satisfies
\textsf{Incoh}(\widehat{X})\leq\sqrt{3\mu_{0}}
and
d_{p}(\widehat{X},U)\leq 4\pi\delta
, where

\overline{X}^{i}=\frac{X^{i}}{\|X^{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}}\cdot\min\{\|X^{i}\|_{\ell_{2}},\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{p}}\},\quad\widehat{X}=\overline{X}(\overline{X}^{T}\overline{X})^{-1/2}.

Appendix C
Proofs of technical lemmas

In this section, we provide the proofs for the technical lemmas.

C.1
Proof of Lemma
2

Since
({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}=T^{*\leq i}\Lambda_{i+1}^{*}V_{i+1}^{*T}
, we have
T^{*\leq i}=({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})^{-1}
. And thus for any
k\in[d_{1}\ldots d_{i}]
,

\displaystyle\|e_{k}^{T}T^{*\leq i}\|_{\ell_{2}}=\|e_{k}^{T}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}^{*}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})^{-1}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})}\|e_{k}^{T}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{d_{i+1}\ldots d_{m}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})}\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}.

And the spikiness condition implies
\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\frac{\nu}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
, and together with
\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{r_{i}}\sigma_{1}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})
, we obtain

\|e_{k}^{T}T^{*\leq i}\|_{\ell_{2}}\frac{\sqrt{d_{1}\ldots d_{i}}}{\sqrt{r_{i}}}\leq\nu\kappa_{0}.

The bound for
\|e_{k}^{T}V_{i+1}^{*}\|_{\ell_{2}}
can be similarly derived. And this finishes the proof.

C.2
Proof of Lemma
10

For simplicity denote the random tensor which is uniformly distributed in
\{{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega}\}_{\omega\in[d^{*}]}
by
{\mathcal{E}}
and let
{\mathcal{E}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathcal{E}}_{n}
be
n
i.i.d. copies of
{\mathcal{E}}
. Also define
\delta_{1,j}=2^{j}\delta_{1}^{-},j=0,\ldots,\lceil\log_{2}(\frac{\delta_{1}^{+}}{\delta_{1}^{-}})\rceil=:j_{0}
,
\delta_{2,k}=2^{k}\delta_{2}^{-},k=0,\ldots,\lceil\log_{2}(\frac{\delta_{2}^{+}}{\delta_{2}^{-}})\rceil=:k_{0}
. For each
j,k
, we derive an upper bound for
\beta_{n}(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2})
with
\gamma_{1}=\delta_{1,j},\gamma_{2}=\delta_{2,k}
.

We observe that

\sup_{{\mathcal{A}}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}}}\left|\langle{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{E}}\rangle^{2}-{\mathbb{E}}\langle{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{E}}\rangle^{2}\right|\leq\gamma_{1}^{2},

and

\sup_{{\mathcal{A}}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}}}\text{Var}\langle{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{E}}\rangle^{2}\leq\sup_{{\mathcal{A}}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}}}\langle{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{E}}\rangle^{4}\leq\frac{\gamma_{1}^{2}\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{d^{*}}\leq\frac{\gamma_{1}^{2}}{d^{*}}.

Now apply Bousquet’s version of Talagrand concentration inequality (see
Giné and Nickl 2021
, Theorem 3.3.9), and we get with probability at least
1-e^{-t}
for any
t>0
,

\beta_{n}(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2})\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}\beta_{n}(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2})+2\gamma_{1}\sqrt{\frac{nt}{d^{*}}}+2\gamma_{1}^{2}t.

Using symmetric inequality, we have

{\mathbb{E}}\beta_{n}(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2})\leq 2n{\mathbb{E}}\sup_{{\mathcal{A}}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\langle{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{E}}_{i}\rangle^{2}\right|,

where
\epsilon_{1},\ldots,\epsilon_{n}
are i.i.d. Rademacher random variables. Since
|\langle{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{E}}\rangle|\leq\gamma_{1}
, we have from contraction inequality

{\mathbb{E}}\beta_{n}(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2})\leq 4n\gamma_{1}{\mathbb{E}}\sup_{{\mathcal{A}}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\langle{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{E}}_{i}\rangle\right|.

Now we denote
{\mathcal{Y}}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}{\mathcal{E}}_{i}
. Then we have

{\mathbb{E}}\sup_{{\mathcal{A}}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\epsilon_{i}\langle{\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{E}}_{i}\rangle\right|\leq{\mathbb{E}}\sup_{{\mathcal{A}}\in{\mathbb{K}}_{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}}}\|{\mathcal{Y}}\|\|{\mathcal{A}}\|_{*}\leq\gamma_{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathcal{Y}}\|.

The estimation for
\|{\mathcal{Y}}\|
derived in the Theorem 1 of
Xia et al. 2021
gives

{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathcal{Y}}\|\leq C_{m}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\overline{d}}{nd^{*}}}+\frac{1}{n}\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d}).

As a result, with probability exceeding
1-e^{-t}
, we have

\displaystyle\beta_{n}(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2})\leq C_{m}\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})+2\gamma_{1}\sqrt{\frac{nt}{d^{*}}}+2\gamma_{1}^{2}t.

(46)

Now we take union bound and we get with probability exceeding

1-2\log_{2}\left(\frac{\delta_{1}^{+}}{\delta_{1}^{-}}\right)\log_{2}\left(\frac{\delta_{2}^{+}}{\delta_{2}^{-}}\right)e^{-t}
,
and for all
\gamma_{1}\in\{\delta_{1,0},\ldots,\delta_{1,j_{0}}\},\gamma_{2}\in\{\delta_{2,0},\ldots,\delta_{2,k_{0}}\}
, (
46
) holds. Now we consider arbitrary
\gamma_{1}\in[\delta_{1}^{-},\delta_{1}^{+}],\gamma_{2}\in[\delta_{2}^{-},\delta_{2}^{+}]
. Then there exists some
j,k
, such that
\gamma_{1}\in[\delta_{1,j-1},\delta_{1,j}],\gamma_{2}\in[\delta_{2,k-1},\delta_{2,k}]
. Together with the fact that
\delta_{1,j}\leq 2\gamma_{1}
and
\delta_{2,k}\leq 2\gamma_{2}
we get

\beta_{n}(\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2})\leq\beta_{n}(\delta_{1,j},\delta_{2,k})\leq C_{m}\gamma_{1}\gamma_{2}\left(\sqrt{\frac{n\overline{d}}{d^{*}}}+1\right)\log^{m+2}(\overline{d})+4\gamma_{1}\sqrt{\frac{nt}{d^{*}}}+8\gamma_{1}^{2}t.

This finishes the proof of the lemma.

C.3
Proof of Lemma
11

Let
\omega\in[d_{1}]\times\ldots\times[d_{m}]
, then

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\{\begin{array}[]{rlc}&\frac{\mu r_{1}d_{1}}{d^{*}},&i=1\\
&\frac{\mu^{2}r_{i-1}r_{i}d_{i}}{d^{*}},&2\leq i\leq m-1\\
&\frac{\mu r_{m-1}d_{m}}{d^{*}},&i=m\end{array}\right.\leq\frac{\mu^{2}\overline{r}^{2}\overline{d}}{d^{*}}.

Set
{\mathcal{Z}}_{j}={\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}({\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{j}}-\frac{1}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}
for all
j\in[n]
, then
{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}=\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathcal{Z}}_{j}
. First for arbitrary
{\mathcal{X}}
, we have

({\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega}{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)})^{2}{\mathcal{X}}=\|{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega}{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{X}}.

Therefore
\|{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega}{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}\|\leq\|{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
and this implies that

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{Z}}_{j}\|\leq\max_{\omega}\|{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{1}{d^{*}}\leq\frac{2\mu^{2}\overline{r}^{2}\overline{d}}{d^{*}}.

On the other hand, since
{\mathcal{Z}}_{j}
is an symmetric operator, we consider
{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathcal{Z}}_{j}^{2}
.

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{j=1}^{n}{\mathcal{Z}}_{j}^{2}\|

\displaystyle=n\|\frac{1}{d^{*}}\sum_{\omega}({\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega}{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)})^{2}-\frac{1}{(d^{*})^{2}}{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n}{d^{*}}\max_{\omega}\|{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}{\mathcal{E}}_{\omega}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{n}{(d^{*})^{2}}\leq\frac{2\mu^{2}\overline{r}^{2}n\overline{d}}{(d^{*})^{2}}.

Now using operator Bernstein inequality, with probability exceeding
1-\overline{d}^{-m}
,

\|{\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{P}}^{(i)}\|\leq C_{m}\left(\frac{\mu^{2}\overline{r}^{2}\overline{d}\log(\overline{d})}{d^{*}}+\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}\overline{r}^{2}\overline{d}n\log(\overline{d})}{(d^{*})^{2}}}\right)\leq C_{m}\sqrt{\frac{\mu^{2}\overline{r}^{2}\overline{d}n\log(\overline{d})}{(d^{*})^{2}}},

where the last inequality holds as long as
n\geq C\mu^{2}\overline{r}^{2}\overline{d}\log(\overline{d})
.

C.4
Proof of Lemma
13

Notice that

\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{A}},{\mathcal{B}}\rangle=\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{J}},{\mathcal{A}}\odot{\mathcal{B}}\rangle\leq\|({\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}-\frac{n}{d^{*}}{\mathcal{I}}){\mathcal{J}}\|\cdot\|{\mathcal{A}}\odot{\mathcal{B}}\|_{*}.

where
{\mathcal{J}}
is the tensor with all its entries one. And from the definition of nuclear norm in (
45
), we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{A}}\odot{\mathcal{B}}\|_{*}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k_{1},\ldots,k_{m-1}\\
k_{1}^{\prime},\ldots,k_{m-1}^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\|A_{1}(:,k_{1})\odot B_{1}(:,k_{1}^{\prime})\|_{\ell_{2}}\ldots\|A_{m}(k_{m-1},:)\odot B_{1}(k_{m-1}^{\prime},:)\|_{\ell_{2}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{x_{1}}\|A_{1}(x_{1},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\|B_{1}(x_{1},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\ldots\sum_{x_{m}}\|A_{m}(:,x_{m})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\|B_{m}(:,x_{m})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last inequality comes from using Cauchy-Schwartz inequality
m-1
times.
Since
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}
holds and

\sum_{x_{i}}\|A_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\|B_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\max_{x_{i}}\|A_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|B_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\wedge\max_{x_{i}}\|B_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot\|A_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

we get the desired result.

C.5
Proof of Lemma
14

Consider for all
i\in[m-1]
, notice that
L(T_{i})=(T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}T^{\leq i}
. We have

L(T_{i})(k_{i-1},x_{i};k_{i})=\sum_{y_{i-1}\in[d_{1}\cdots d_{i-1}]}T^{\leq i-1}(y_{i-1},k_{i})T^{\leq i}(y_{i-1},x_{i};k_{i}).

This implies

\|T_{i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|(T^{\leq i-1})^{T}\cdot T^{\leq i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|T^{\leq i}(:,x_{i},:)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\frac{\mu r_{i}}{d_{i}},

where
T^{\leq i}(:,x_{i},:)
is viewed as a matrix of size
d_{1}\cdots d_{i-1}\times r_{i}
by extracting
d_{1}\cdots d_{i-1}
rows of
T^{\leq i}
. And since the decomposition is left orthogonal, we have
\|T_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|L(T_{i})\|_{\rm{F}}=r_{i}
.

When
i=m
, since
T_{m}=\Lambda_{m}R_{m}^{T}
for some
\Lambda_{m}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{m-1}\times r_{m-1}}
invertible and orthogonal
R_{m}
with
\textsf{Incoh}(R_{m})\leq\sqrt{\mu}
. So we have

\max_{x_{m}}\|T_{m}(:,x_{m})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\sigma_{\max}^{2}({\mathcal{T}})\frac{\mu r_{m-1}}{d_{m}}.

And
\|{\mathcal{T}}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|T^{\leq m-1}T_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|T_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}
since
T^{\leq m-1}
is orthogonal.

C.6
Proof of Lemma
18

We first introduce some notations. Denote
{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)=\sum_{\omega_{i}\in\Omega}M_{\omega_{i}}E_{\omega_{i}}
and
{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{\prime}}(M)=\sum_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}\in\Omega}M_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}
, and their mean zero version
\Delta=\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)-M
,
\Delta^{\prime}=\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{\prime}}(M)-M
denote also
\Xi=\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\xi_{i}U^{T}E_{\omega_{i}}
and
\Xi^{\prime}=\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{i}^{\prime}U^{T}E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}
. Also we will encounter norms
\|A\|_{\infty}=\max_{i,j}|A_{ij}|
and
\|\cdot\|_{k,\infty}
for
k=1,2
defined as follows

\|A\|_{k,\infty}^{k}:=\max_{i\in[p_{1}]}\sum_{j=1}^{p_{2}}|A_{ij}|^{k}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\quad\|\frac{1}{2n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}(U^{T}X_{i}Y_{j}^{T}U+U^{T}Y_{j}X_{i}^{T}U)-U^{T}MM^{T}U\|

\displaystyle=\|\frac{(p_{1}p_{2})^{2}}{2n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}(M_{\omega_{i}}M_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}+M_{\omega_{i}}\xi_{j}^{\prime}+\xi_{i}M_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}+\xi_{i}\xi_{j}^{\prime})U^{T}(E_{\omega_{i}}E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}^{T}+E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}E_{\omega_{i}}^{T})U-U^{T}MM^{T}U\|

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\|\frac{(p_{1}p_{2})^{2}}{2n^{2}}(U^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M){\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{\prime}}(M)^{T}U+U^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{\prime}}(M){\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)^{T}U)-U^{T}MM^{T}U\|}_{\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{2n}\|U^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)\Xi^{\prime T}U+U^{T}\Xi^{\prime}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)^{T}U\|}_{\beta_{2}}+\underbrace{\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{2n}\|U^{T}\Xi{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{\prime}}(M)^{T}U+U^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega^{\prime}}(M)\Xi^{T}U\|}_{\beta_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\|\Xi\Xi^{\prime T}+\Xi^{\prime}\Xi^{T}\|}_{\beta_{4}}.

(47)

Notice
\beta_{1}
can be controlled using Lemma
35
. And
\beta_{2}
and
\beta_{3}
can be bounded similarly.

We begin with several preliminary facts which can be easily proved by matrix Bernstein inequalities (Theorem
30
). We have with probability exceeding
1-2p^{-\alpha}
, where
p=\max\{p_{1},p_{2}\}
and for any
\alpha\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\max\{\|\Xi\|,\|\Xi^{\prime}\|\}\leq C\alpha\left(\sqrt{\frac{p_{1}p_{2}(p_{2}\vee r)}{n}\log p}\sigma_{s}+\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{p_{1}}}\log p\sigma_{s}\right).

(48)

Meanwhile, using Theorem
30
, we also have with probability exceeding
1-2p^{-\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\max\{\|U^{T}M\Xi^{T}\|,\|U^{T}M\Xi^{\prime T}\|\}\leq C\alpha\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mu rp_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}}{n}\log p}+\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\mu r\log p\right)\|M\|_{\infty}\sigma_{s}.

(49)

Notice

\beta_{2}\leq\|U^{T}\Delta\Xi^{\prime T}\|+\|U^{T}M\Xi^{\prime T}\|
.
And
U^{T}\Delta\Xi^{\prime T}=\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\sum_{j}U^{T}\Delta\xi_{j}^{\prime}E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}^{T}U
. Let
\Omega=\{(i_{k},j_{k})\}_{k=1}^{n}
, then using Chernoff bound, with probability exceeding
1-n^{-\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\max_{l\in[p_{2}]}\sum_{k=1}^{n}1(j_{k}=l)\leq(3\alpha+7)(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p)).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\max_{l\in[p_{2}]}\|U^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)e_{l}\|_{\ell_{2}}=\max_{l\in[p_{2}]}\|U^{T}\sum_{k=1}^{n}M_{i_{k},j_{k}}e_{i_{k}}e_{j_{k}}^{T}e_{l}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq(3\alpha+7)(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))\|M\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{p_{1}}}.

Meanwhile,

\displaystyle\max_{l\in[p_{2}]}\|U^{T}Me_{l}\|_{\ell_{2}}=\max_{l\in[p_{2}]}\|U^{T}\sum_{i,j}M_{i,j}e_{i}e_{j}^{T}e_{l}\|_{\ell_{2}}=\max_{l\in[p_{2}]}\|U^{T}\sum_{i}M_{i,l}e_{i}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq p_{1}\|M\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{p_{1}}}.

Using these two bounds on
\|M^{T}U\|_{2,\infty}
and
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)^{T}U\|_{2,\infty}
, we get

\displaystyle\|U^{T}\Delta\xi_{j}^{\prime}E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}^{T}U\|

\displaystyle\leq\|U\|_{2,\infty}\cdot\|(\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)-M)^{T}U\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|U\|_{2,\infty}\cdot(\|M^{T}U\|_{2,\infty}+\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)^{T}U\|_{2,\infty})

\displaystyle\leq(3\alpha+8)(1+\frac{p_{2}}{n}\log p)\mu r\|M\|_{\infty}.

where in the fourth line we use Lemma
21
,
\|\cdot\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\|\cdot\|_{\ell_{1}}
and Chernoff bound. Therefore we obtain

\left\|\|\xi_{j}^{\prime}U^{T}(\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)-M)\xi_{j}^{\prime}E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}^{T}U\|\right\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq(3\alpha+8)(1+\frac{p_{2}}{n}\log p)\mu r\|M\|_{\infty}\sigma_{s}.

On the other hand, notice with probability
1-p^{-\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\max\{\|{\mathbb{E}}\xi_{j}^{\prime 2}U^{T}\Delta E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}^{T}UU^{T}E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}\Delta^{T}U\|,\|{\mathbb{E}}\xi_{j}^{\prime 2}U^{T}E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}\Delta^{T}UU^{T}\Delta E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}^{T}U\|\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{r\sigma_{s}^{2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\|U^{T}\Delta\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\alpha^{2}\frac{r\sigma_{s}^{2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\left(\frac{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}\mu r\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}\log^{2}(p)}{n^{2}}+\frac{p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}(\mu r\vee p_{2})\log(p)}{n}\right),

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
36
. Therefore when
n\leq p_{1}p_{2}
, we have with probability exceeding
1-p^{-\alpha}
,

\|U^{T}\Delta\Xi^{\prime T}\|\leq C_{\alpha}\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\|M\|_{\infty}\sigma_{s}\left(\mu r\log(p)+\frac{p_{2}}{n}\mu r\log^{2}(p)\right).

Together with (
49
), we see with probability exceeding
1-3p^{-\alpha}-n^{-\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\beta_{2}\leq C_{\alpha}\|M\|_{\infty}\sigma_{s}\left(\mu r\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\log(p)+\mu r\frac{p_{1}p_{2}^{2}}{n^{2}}\log^{2}(p)+\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\mu r\log(p)}\right).

(50)

And we can bound
\beta_{3}
similarly.

Now we consider
\beta_{4}
. For any fixed
\Xi^{\prime}
, we need to control
\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\|\sum_{i}\xi_{i}U^{T}E_{\omega_{i}}\Xi^{\prime T}\|
.
Notice using Chernoff bound and the fact that
\max_{i}|\xi_{i}|\leq C\alpha\sigma_{s}\log^{1/2}(n)
with probability exceeding
1-n^{-\alpha}
, we have with probability exceeding
1-2n^{-\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\|\Xi^{\prime T}\|_{2,\infty}=\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\max_{l\in[p_{2}]}\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\xi_{j}^{\prime}U^{T}E_{\omega_{j}^{\prime}}e_{l}\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq C\alpha(3\alpha+7)\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))\sigma_{s}\log^{1/2}(n)\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{p_{1}}}.

Therefore

\displaystyle\|U^{T}E_{\omega_{i}}\Xi^{\prime T}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|U\|_{2,\infty}\cdot\|\Xi^{\prime T}\|_{2,\infty}\leq C\alpha\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))\sigma_{s}\log^{1/2}(n)\frac{\mu r}{p_{1}}

where in the last inequality of the first line we use Lemma
21
.
And thus

\left\|\|\xi_{i}U^{T}E_{\omega_{i}}\Xi^{\prime T}\|\right\|_{\psi_{2}}\leq C_{\alpha}\frac{\mu r}{p_{1}}\sigma_{s}^{2}\log^{1/2}(n)\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p)).

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\|\sum_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\xi_{i}^{2}U^{T}E_{\omega_{i}}\Xi^{\prime T}\Xi^{\prime}E_{\omega_{i}}^{T}U\|=\frac{\sigma_{s}^{2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\|\sum_{k,l}U^{T}e_{k}e_{l}^{T}\Xi^{\prime T}\Xi^{\prime}e_{l}e_{k}^{T}U\|=\frac{\sigma_{s}^{2}}{p_{1}p_{2}}\|\|\Xi^{\prime}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\cdot I\|\leq\frac{\sigma_{s}^{2}r}{p_{1}p_{2}}\|\Xi^{\prime}\|^{2},

and similarly
\frac{1}{n}\|\sum_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\xi_{i}^{2}\Xi^{\prime}E_{\omega_{i}}^{T}UU^{T}E_{\omega_{i}}\Xi^{\prime T}\|\leq\frac{\sigma_{s}^{2}r}{p_{1}p_{2}}\|\Xi^{\prime}\|^{2}
. Now from (
49
) and Theorem
30
, when
n\leq p_{1}p_{2}
, we have with probability exceeding
1-2n^{-\alpha}-2p^{-\alpha}
,

\displaystyle\beta_{4}\leq C_{\alpha}\sigma_{s}^{2}\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}\left(\mu r\log^{3/2}(p)+\mu r\log^{5/2}(p)\frac{\sqrt{p_{2}}\sqrt{p_{2}\vee r}}{n}\right).

(51)

We get the desired result combining (
50
), (
51
) and Lemma
35
.

C.7
Proof of Lemma
26

Denote
\widehat{\mathcal{T}}=\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}({\mathcal{T}})=[\widehat{T}_{1},\ldots,\widehat{T}_{m}]
. Let
{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=[T_{1}^{**},\ldots,T_{m}^{**}]
be a TT decomposition that is left orthogonal and
R_{i}=\arg\min_{R\in{\mathbb{O}}_{r_{i}}}\|(T^{**})^{\leq i}R-\widehat{T}^{\leq i}\|_{\rm{F}}
. Now we set
L(T_{i}^{*})=(R_{i-1}\otimes I)^{T}L(T_{i}^{**})R_{i}
. Then
{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=[T_{1}^{*},\ldots,T_{m}^{*}]
is another left orthogonal decomposition so that
T^{*\leq i}
and
\widehat{T}^{\leq i}
are well aligned in terms of chordal distance. Also, let
({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}=T^{*\leq i}\Lambda_{i+1}V_{i+1}^{*T}
be such that
V_{i+1}^{*T}V_{i+1}^{*}=I_{r_{i}}
and
\Lambda_{i+1}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{i}\times r_{i}}
be invertible.

From Algorithm
1
, we have
(\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1})^{T}{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}=\widehat{T}_{m}
. Now using the notations
\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i}=\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1}\widehat{T}^{\leq m-1T}
and
{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*}=T^{*\leq i}T^{*\leq iT}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}({\mathcal{T}})

\displaystyle-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|\widehat{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}-({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}-I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}+\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\|(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}+{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}+(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}\|\underbrace{(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{m-1})\Delta_{m-1}V_{m}^{*}V_{m}^{*T}+{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}}_{(\textup{I}.1)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\underbrace{H(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1},{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}+(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}}_{\text{high order terms}=:(\textup{I}.2)}\|_{\rm{F}},

(52)

where in
(a)
we use the fact that
(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}=0
and
(b)
follows from the following equation when
i=m-1
, which is a result of Lemma
32
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i}-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i}=T^{*\leq i}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})^{-1}(V_{i+1}^{*})^{T}\Delta_{i}^{T}(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i})+(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i})\Delta_{i}V_{i+1}^{*}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})^{-1}(T^{*\leq i})^{T}+H(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*}),

and since
T^{*\leq i}
is the top
r_{i}
left singular vectors of
({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}
and
\widehat{T}^{\leq i}
is the top
r_{i}
left singular vectors of
(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I){\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}
, so

\Delta_{i}=(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I){\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}-({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}
, and thus

\displaystyle\Delta_{i}

\displaystyle=\left((\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}+\left(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}

\displaystyle=\left((\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}+\left({\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}+\left((\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}

\displaystyle=\left((I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\Delta_{i-1}V_{i}^{*}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})^{-1}(T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}+\left({\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}

\displaystyle~~~~+\underbrace{\left(H(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1},{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}+\left((\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}}_{\text{high order terms}=:H_{i}}

\displaystyle=:L_{i}+H_{i},

(53)

here we use
L_{i}
and
H_{i}
to denote the leading terms and high order terms of
\Delta_{i}
respectively. Now we derive first the upper bound for
\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}
.

Bound for
\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}
.
Notice by triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|\left(H(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1},{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|\left((\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}.

From the assumption we have
\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})\geq 8\|\Delta_{i-1}\|_{\rm{F}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\left(H(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1},{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{12\|\Delta_{i-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})},

and

\displaystyle\|\left((\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{4\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}\|\Delta_{i-1}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})}.

So combine these two estimations, and we have

\displaystyle\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{12\|\Delta_{i-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})}+\frac{4\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}\|\Delta_{i-1}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})}.

(54)

Upper bound for
\|\Delta_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}
.

We first show the following for all
2\leq i\leq m-1
.

\displaystyle~~~~\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})\Delta_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\Delta_{i-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})L_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(55)

As a consequence of
{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*}=(U^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T}((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}})
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*}\left((I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\Delta_{i-1}V_{i}^{*}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})^{-1}(T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}=0.

So the leading term of
(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})\Delta_{i}
is

\displaystyle~~~~(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})L_{i}=\left((I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\Delta_{i-1}V_{i}^{*}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})^{-1}(T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}+(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i})\left({\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\left((I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\Delta_{i-1}V_{i}^{*}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})^{-1}(T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}

\displaystyle\qquad+(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}

(56)

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}\textsf{reshape}\left((I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\Delta_{i-1}V_{i}^{*}V_{i}^{*T}\right)+(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle},

where in
(a)
we use

\displaystyle~~~~(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i})\left({\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle=\left(I-(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T}((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}})\right)\left(T^{*\leq i-1}(T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}\right)

\displaystyle=T^{*\leq i-1}(T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}-(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T}(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})^{T}

\displaystyle=(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})^{T}.

And in
(b)
we use Lemma
20
.
Notice the two terms in (
56
) are mutually orthogonal, so we have

\displaystyle~~~~\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})L_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\Delta_{i-1}V_{i}^{*}V_{i}^{*T}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i-1})\Delta_{i-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(57)

Meanwhile, from (
C.7
), we have

\displaystyle\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})\Delta_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})L_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})L_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(58)

Combine (
C.7
), (
58
) and we get (
C.7
).

Upper bound for
\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*})\Delta_{k}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle k\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
.

From the recurrence relation (
C.7
), we have

\displaystyle~~~~\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*})\Delta_{k}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle k\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle k\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\sum_{i=2}^{k}\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{1}^{*}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle 1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}+\underbrace{\sum_{i=2}^{k}\left(2\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})L_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right)}_{\text{high order terms}=:\xi_{k,2}}

\displaystyle=:\xi_{k,1}+\xi_{k,2}.

Now we first show
\xi_{k,1}=\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
. The key point of the proof lies in the following equation:

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

(59)

Suppose this holds, then we have the left hand side is equal to
\|{\mathcal{P}}_{1}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle 1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{1}^{*}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle 1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}
and this finishes the proof. So now we verify (
59
).

LHS of
(
59
)

\displaystyle=\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T}((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle~~~~+\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})(I-L(T_{i}^{*})L(T_{i}^{*})^{T})((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|(T^{*\leq i-1}\otimes I_{d_{i}})((T^{*\leq i-1})^{T}\otimes I_{d_{i}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}\|{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle i-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where in
(a)
we use Lemma
20
.

On the other hand, for
\xi_{k,2}
, we have from (
C.7
),
\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})L_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 3\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}
.
And from (
54
), we have

\displaystyle\|H_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{12\|\Delta_{i-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})}+\frac{4\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}\|\Delta_{i-1}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})}\leq\frac{56\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})}.

Combine the above two estimations, and we have

\displaystyle\xi_{k,2}\leq\sum_{i=2}^{k}\left(2\cdot 3\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}\frac{56\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})}+\frac{56^{2}\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})}\right)\leq\frac{350(k-1)\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\underline{\sigma}}.

And thus

\displaystyle\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*})\Delta_{k}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle k\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{350(k-1)\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\underline{\sigma}}.

(60)

Upper bound for
\|\Delta_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}
.

We shall bound
\|\Delta_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}
by induction.
For the base case when
i=1
, we have
\|\Delta_{1}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle 1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}=\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 2\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}
. Now suppose we have the bound for
\|\Delta_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 2\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}
for all
1\leq i\leq k
.
Then from the definition of
L_{k+1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|L_{k+1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*})\Delta_{k}V_{k+1}^{*}V_{k+1}^{*T}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|({\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*}\otimes I_{d_{k+1}}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle k+1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*})\Delta_{k}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{P}}_{k}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle k\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{350(k-1)\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{k}^{*})},

where
(a)
follows from (
60
).
And the upper bound for
\|H_{k+1}\|_{\rm{F}}
is already derived as in (
54
),
since
\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{k+1}^{*})\geq C_{1}\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq 8\|\Delta_{k}\|_{\rm{F}}
,
we have

\displaystyle\|H_{k+1}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{12\|\Delta_{k}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{k+1}^{*})}+\frac{4\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}\|\Delta_{k}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{k+1}^{*})}\leq\frac{56\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{k+1}^{*})}.

From (
C.7
), we have

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{k+1}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|L_{k+1}+H_{k+1}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\sqrt{1+\frac{350(k-1)\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{k+1}^{*})}}\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}+\frac{56\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{k+1}^{*})}\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 2\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}.

And this finishes the induction. So it holds for all
i\in[m-1]
that
\|\Delta_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq 2\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}
.

Estimation of
\|\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}({\mathcal{T}})-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
.
Now we go back to (
C.7
) and we bound
\|(\textup{I}.1)\|_{\rm{F}}
and
\|(\textup{I}.2)\|_{\rm{F}}
separately. For the term
\|(\textup{I}.2)\|_{\rm{F}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|(\textup{I}.2)\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|H(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1},{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}+(\widehat{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*}){\mathcal{D}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}\overset{(a)}{=}\|H_{m}\|_{\rm{F}},

where
(a)
follows from (
C.7
) and Lemma
20
. So from (
54
), we have

\displaystyle\|(\textup{I}.2)\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{12\|\Delta_{m-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{m}^{*})}+\frac{4\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}\|\Delta_{m-1}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{m}^{*})}\leq\frac{56\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{m}^{*})}.

And for the term
\|(\textup{I}.1)\|_{\rm{F}}
, we have from (
60
),

\displaystyle\|(\textup{I}.1)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*})\Delta_{m-1}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}^{*}{\mathcal{D}}^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{350(m-2)\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{m-1}^{*})}

So together with (
C.7
), we have

\displaystyle\|\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}({\mathcal{T}})-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\textup{I}.1)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+2\|(\textup{I}.1)\|_{\rm{F}}\|(\textup{I}.2)\|_{\rm{F}}+\|(\textup{I}.2)\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{350(m-2)\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{m-1}^{*})}+\frac{250\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{m}^{*})}

\displaystyle\leq\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{600m\|{\mathcal{D}}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\underline{\sigma}}.

And this finishes the proof of the lemma.

C.8
Proof of Lemma
27

First we introduce some notations,

{\mathcal{T}}=[T_{1},\ldots,T_{m}],{\mathcal{T}}^{*}=[T_{1}^{*},\ldots,T_{m}^{*}],\text{~and~}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}=T^{*\leq i}\Lambda_{i+1}^{*}V^{*}_{i+1},{\mathcal{T}}^{\langle i\rangle}=T^{\leq i}\Lambda_{i+1}V_{i+1},i\in[m-1].

Also we denote
{\mathcal{P}}_{i}=T^{\leq i}T^{\leq iT}
,
{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}=V_{i+1}V_{i+1}^{T}
as shorthand notations, and define similar notations for
{\mathcal{T}}^{*}
. Now we take
{\mathcal{A}}={\mathcal{T}}^{*}
and denote
{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})=\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{1}+\ldots+\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{m}
, then we have for all
i\in[m-1]

\displaystyle(\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{i})^{\langle i\rangle}

\displaystyle=(T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)(I-L(T_{i})L(T_{i})^{T})(T^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}V_{i+1}V_{i+1}^{T}

\displaystyle=(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i})({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}.

And

\displaystyle-\|\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=-\langle(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i})({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1},(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i})({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i})({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}\rangle+\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle+\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~-\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\rangle+\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\rangle

\displaystyle=-\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},(({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~+\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~-\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\rangle+\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\rangle

Meanwhile, when
i=m
, we have

\displaystyle-\|\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{m}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=-\|({\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=-\|{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

where the last equality is from Lemma
20
.
Now we first estimate

\displaystyle~~~~\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\left(-\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\rangle+\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\rangle\right)-\|{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\left(-\|({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{P}}_{i}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right)-\|{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\left(-\|{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\|{\mathcal{P}}_{i}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\right)-\|{\mathcal{P}}_{m-1}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle m-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=-\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

And now we estimate
\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle
first,

\displaystyle~~~~\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle({\mathcal{P}}_{i}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*}\Delta_{i}(I-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})+({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}H({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*}),(I-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})\Delta_{i}{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*}+H({\mathcal{P}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}H({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*}),H({\mathcal{P}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\|({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}H({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\|H({\mathcal{P}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

Notice here we use

\displaystyle{\mathcal{P}}_{i}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*}

\displaystyle=T^{*\leq i}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})^{-1}(V_{i+1}^{*})^{T}\Delta_{i}^{T}(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i})+(I-{\mathcal{P}}^{*}_{i})\Delta_{i}V_{i+1}^{*}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})^{-1}(T^{*\leq i})^{T}+H({\mathcal{P}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*}),

and

\displaystyle{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*}

\displaystyle=V_{i+1}^{*}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})^{-1}(T^{*\leq i})^{T}\Delta_{i}(I-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})+(I-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\Delta_{i}^{T}T^{*\leq i}(\Lambda_{i+1}^{*})^{-1}V_{i+1}^{*T}

\displaystyle~~~~+H({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*}),

where
\Delta_{i}=({\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}
and
H(\cdot,\cdot)
denotes high order terms.

Similarly for
\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},(({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle
, we have

\displaystyle~~~~\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},(({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}H({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*}),(H({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1},{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq\|({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}H({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\|(H({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1},{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle=\|({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}H({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}\|H({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1},{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i-1\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

Now as long as
\underline{\sigma}\geq 8\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|H({\mathcal{P}}_{i},{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{12\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}},

\displaystyle\|({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}H({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1},{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{12\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}}{\underline{\sigma}}.

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{P}}_{{\mathbb{T}}}^{\perp}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}=\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}-\sum_{i=1}^{m}\|\delta{\mathcal{T}}_{i}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{m-1}\big(-\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},(({\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i-1}^{*})\otimes I)({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~~~+\langle({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle},({\mathcal{P}}_{i}-{\mathcal{P}}_{i}^{*})({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}({\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}-{\mathcal{Q}}_{i+1}^{*})\rangle\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{288m^{2}\|{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{4}}{\underline{\sigma}^{2}}.

And this finishes the proof.

C.9
Proof of Lemma
29

We begin the proof introducing some notations. For simplicity, we denote
\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}=\textsf{Trim}_{\zeta}({\mathcal{W}})
. And
\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}_{{\boldsymbol{r}}}(\widetilde{\mathcal{W}})=\widehat{\mathcal{W}}=[\widehat{W}_{1},\ldots,\widehat{W}_{m}]
. And we denote for all
i\in[m-1]
,
\widehat{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}=\widehat{W}^{\leq i}\widehat{W}^{\geq i+1}=\widehat{W}^{\leq i}\Sigma_{i+1}N_{i+1}^{T}
where
\Sigma_{i+1}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{i}\times r_{i}}
is invertible and
N_{i+1}^{T}N_{i+1}=I_{r_{i}}
. And we also introduce some notations for the process of TTSVD. From Algorithm
1
, we know
(\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\otimes I)^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}=L(\widehat{W}_{i})S_{i+1}R_{i+1}^{T}+E_{i}
is how we get the estimation
\widehat{W}_{i}
once we know
\widehat{W}_{1},\ldots,\widehat{W}_{i-1}
, where
S_{i+1}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{r_{i}\times r_{i}}
is invertible and
R_{i+1}^{T}R_{i+1}=I_{r_{i}}
. To estimate the incoherence of
\widehat{\mathcal{W}}
, we check
\max_{j}\|e_{j}^{T}\widehat{W}^{\leq i}\|_{\ell_{2}}
and
\max_{j}\|N_{i+1}^{T}e_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}
.

We first estimate
\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma_{i+1})
and
\sigma_{\min}(S_{i+1})
. From the Algorithm
1
, we know

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}(S_{i+1})

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\min}((\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}\otimes I)\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle})

\displaystyle\geq\sigma_{\min}(({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle})-\|({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}-(\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}\otimes I)\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}.

So we need to derive an upper bound for
\|({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}-(\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}\otimes I)\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}
.

\displaystyle(\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}\otimes I)\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}-({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}

\displaystyle=(\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}-T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T})\otimes I\cdot(\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}-({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle})

\displaystyle~~~~+T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T}\otimes I\cdot(\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}-({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle})

\displaystyle~~~~+(\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}-T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T})\otimes I\cdot({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}.

(61)

Since
\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}=\textsf{Spiki}({\mathcal{T}}^{*})\frac{\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\leq\nu\frac{\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}\leq\frac{10\nu}{9}\frac{\|{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d^{*}}}=\zeta
, we have
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}
. And the bound for
\|\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}-T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T}\|
goes as follows. First we notice that from Lemma
26
,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{600m\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\underline{\sigma}}\leq 2\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2},

(62)

where the last inequality is from the assumption
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{1}{600m}\underline{\sigma}
. So we have from Wedin’s theorem,

\displaystyle\|\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}-T^{*\leq i-1}T^{*\leq i-1T}\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{2}\|\widehat{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})-\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}\leq\frac{4\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\sigma}}\leq\frac{1}{150m\sqrt{\overline{r}}\kappa_{0}},

where in the penultimate inequality we use
\sigma_{\min}(\Lambda_{i}^{*})-\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{2}
and (
62
). This together with (
C.9
) gives

\displaystyle\|({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle}-(\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}

\displaystyle\otimes I)\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\sigma}}\cdot\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\frac{4\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\underline{\sigma}}\cdot\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\underline{\sigma}}{10}.

So we conclude that
\sigma_{\min}(S_{i+1})\geq\frac{9}{10}\underline{\sigma}
.

Meanwhile, for
\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma_{i+1})
and
\sigma_{\max}(\Sigma_{i+1})
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}(\Sigma_{i+1})=\sigma_{\max}(\widehat{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle})\leq\sigma_{\max}(({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle})+\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm{F}}\leq\frac{11}{10}\overline{\sigma},

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma_{i+1})=\sigma_{\min}(\widehat{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle})\geq\sigma_{\min}(({\mathcal{T}}^{*})^{\langle i\rangle})-\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}-{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm{F}}\geq\frac{9}{10}\underline{\sigma}.

With these preparations, we are ready to bound the incoherence of
\widehat{\mathcal{W}}
. For all
j\in[d_{1}\ldots d_{i}]
,

\displaystyle\|e_{j}^{T}\widehat{W}^{\leq i}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle=\|e_{j}^{T}(\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}\otimes I)\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}R_{i+1}S_{i+1}^{-1}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|f_{j}^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}\|_{\ell_{2}}}{\sigma_{\min}(S_{i+1})}\overset{(a)}{\leq}\frac{10\|f_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}\sqrt{d_{i+1}\ldots d_{m}}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}}{9\underline{\sigma}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}\frac{100}{81\underline{\sigma}}\frac{\nu\|{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d_{1}\ldots d_{i}}}\leq\frac{100\nu}{81\underline{\sigma}}\frac{\|{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}+\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d_{1}\ldots d_{i}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{100\nu}{81}\kappa_{0}\frac{\sqrt{r_{i}}}{\sqrt{d_{1}\ldots d_{i}}}+\frac{10\nu}{81}\frac{1}{\sqrt{d_{1}\ldots d_{i}}}\leq\frac{110\nu\kappa_{0}}{81}\frac{\sqrt{r_{i}}}{\sqrt{d_{1}\ldots d_{i}}},

(63)

where
f_{j}=(\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1}\widehat{W}^{\leq i-1T}\otimes I)e_{j}
and in
(a)
we use the estimation for
\sigma_{\min}(S_{i+1})
and
\|Wx\|_{\ell_{2}}\leq\sqrt{n}\|W\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\|x\|_{\ell_{2}}
for some matrix
W\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times m}
; in
(b)
we use
\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq\zeta=\frac{10\|{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm{F}}}{9\sqrt{d^{*}}}\nu
and
\|f_{j}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\leq 1
.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{W}^{\geq i+1}=R(\widehat{W}_{i+1})(I\otimes\widehat{W}^{\geq i+2}).

(64)

Here we use the convention that
\widehat{W}^{m+1}=[1]
. And from
L(\widehat{W}_{i+1})=(\widehat{W}^{\leq i}\otimes I)^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i+1\rangle}R_{i+2}S_{i+2}^{-1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle R(\widehat{W}_{i+1})=(\widehat{W}^{\leq i})^{T}\textsf{reshape}(\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i+1\rangle}R_{i+2}S_{i+2}^{-1})=(\widehat{W}^{\leq i})^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}(I\otimes R_{i+2}S_{i+2}^{-1}).

(65)

Combine (
64
) and (
65
), we have

\widehat{W}^{\geq i+1}=(\widehat{W}^{\leq i})^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}(I\otimes R_{i+2}S_{i+2}^{-1}\widehat{W}^{\geq i+2}),

which implies

N_{i+1}^{T}=\Sigma_{i+1}^{-1}(\widehat{W}^{\leq i})^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}(I\otimes R_{i+2}S_{i+2}^{-1}\widehat{W}^{\geq i+2}).

Now for any
j\in[d_{i+1}\ldots d_{m}]
, we have

\displaystyle\|N_{i+1}^{T}e_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle=\|\Sigma_{i+1}^{-1}(\widehat{W}^{\leq i})^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}(I\otimes R_{i+2}S_{i+2}^{-1}\widehat{W}^{\geq i+2})e_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma_{i+1})}\|(\widehat{W}^{\leq i})^{T}\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}(I\otimes R_{i+2}S_{i+2}^{-1}\widehat{W}^{\geq i+2})e_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma_{i+1})}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}^{\langle i\rangle}(I\otimes R_{i+2}S_{i+2}^{-1}\widehat{W}^{\geq i+2})e_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{d_{1}\ldots d_{i}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma_{i+1})}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\|(I\otimes R_{i+2}S_{i+2}^{-1}\widehat{W}^{\geq i+2})e_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{d_{1}\ldots d_{i}}}{\sigma_{\min}(\Sigma_{i+1})}\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\ell_{\infty}}\frac{\sigma_{\max}(\Sigma_{i+2})}{\sigma_{\min}(S_{i+2})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1100}{729\underline{\sigma}}\kappa_{0}\frac{\nu\|{\mathcal{W}}\|_{\rm{F}}}{\sqrt{d_{i+1}\ldots d_{m}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1210}{729}\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu\frac{\sqrt{r_{i}}}{\sqrt{d_{i+1}\ldots d_{m}}}.

(66)

From (
C.9
) and (
C.9
), we have

\textsf{Incoh}(\widehat{\mathcal{W}})\leq 2\kappa_{0}^{2}\nu.

Now we consider the second part, from Lemma
26
, we see that

\displaystyle\|\textsf{SVD}^{\textsf{tt}}(\widetilde{\mathcal{W}})-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}+\frac{600m\|\widetilde{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{3}}{\underline{\sigma}}\leq 2\|\widetilde{\mathcal{T}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}\leq 2\|{\mathcal{W}}-{\mathcal{T}}^{*}\|_{\rm{F}}^{2}.

And this finishes the proof of the lemma.

C.10
Proof of Lemma
35

For simplicity, we denote

S_{1k}=\sum_{i=1}^{k}U^{T}X_{i},\quad S_{2k}=\sum_{i=1}^{k}U^{T}Y_{i}\quad\text{and }\quad\Delta_{1k}=S_{1k}-kU^{T}M,\quad\Delta_{2k}=S_{2k}-kU^{T}M.

First we estimate
\|\frac{1}{n}S_{1n}-U^{T}M\|
. Notice that

\frac{1}{n}S_{1n}-U^{T}M=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(p_{1}p_{2}U^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{i}}(M)-U^{T}M\right)=:\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}.

And the uniform bound for
p_{1}p_{2}U^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{i}}(M)
is given by

\|p_{1}p_{2}U^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{i}}(M)\|\leq p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}\sqrt{\frac{\mu r}{p_{1}}}=\sqrt{\mu p_{2}r}\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}\|M\|_{\infty}.

and
\|U^{T}M\|\leq\|M\|\leq\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}\|M\|_{\infty}
. So

\|Z_{i}\|\leq 2\sqrt{\mu p_{2}r}\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}\|M\|_{\infty}
.
On the other hand we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}Z_{i}Z_{i}^{T}

\displaystyle\leq p_{1}p_{2}\sum_{(j,k)\in[p_{1}]\times[p_{2}]}M_{jk}^{2}U^{T}e_{j}e_{j}^{T}U

\displaystyle\leq p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}\sum_{(j,k)\in[p_{1}]\times[p_{2}]}U^{T}e_{j}e_{j}^{T}U

\displaystyle=p_{1}p_{2}^{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}I_{r}

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}Z_{i}^{T}Z_{i}

\displaystyle\leq p_{1}p_{2}\sum_{(j,k)\in[p_{1}]\times[p_{2}]}M_{jk}^{2}e_{k}e_{j}^{T}UU^{T}e_{j}e_{k}^{T}

\displaystyle\leq p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}\sum_{k}\sum_{j}\|U^{T}e_{j}\|_{\ell_{2}}^{2}e_{k}e_{k}^{T}

\displaystyle\leq p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}\mu rI_{p_{2}}.

Therefore we have

\max\{\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}Z_{i}^{T}\|,\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}^{T}Z_{i}\|\}\leq np_{1}p_{2}(\mu r\vee p_{2})\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}
. And from operator Bernstein inequality, with probability exceeding
1-p^{-\alpha}
,

\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}\|\leq\sqrt{\frac{8(\alpha+1)\mu p_{1}p_{2}^{2}r\log(p)}{3n}}\|M\|_{\infty}.

Now set the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}
as

\left\{\|\Delta_{1n}\|\leq\sqrt{\frac{8n(\alpha+1)\mu p_{1}p_{2}^{2}r\log(p)}{3}}\|M\|_{\infty}\right\}\cap\left\{\|\Delta_{2n}\|\leq\sqrt{\frac{8n(\alpha+1)\mu p_{1}p_{2}^{2}r\log(p)}{3}}\|M\|_{\infty}\right\}

and we know
{\mathbb{P}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1})\geq 1-2p^{-\alpha}
.
Also, define the event

\displaystyle{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}

\displaystyle=\left\{\max_{j\in[p_{2}]}\sum_{\omega\in\Omega_{1}}1(\omega_{2}=j)\leq(3\alpha+7)(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))\right\}

\displaystyle~~~~\cap\left\{\max_{j\in[p_{2}]}\sum_{\omega\in\Omega_{2}}1(\omega_{2}=j)\leq(3\alpha+7)(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))\right\}.

From Chernoff bound, we know
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}
holds with probability exceeding
1-2p^{-\alpha}
.
From triangular inequality, we have

\displaystyle~~~~\|\frac{1}{2n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}(U^{T}X_{i}Y_{j}^{T}U+U^{T}Y_{j}X_{i}^{T}U)-U^{T}MM^{T}U\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2n^{2}}\|\Delta_{1n}\Delta_{2n}^{T}+\Delta_{2n}\Delta_{1n}^{T}\|+\frac{1}{2n}\|\Delta_{2n}M^{T}U+U^{T}M\Delta_{2n}^{T}\|+\frac{1}{2n}\|\Delta_{1n}M^{T}U+U^{T}M\Delta_{1n}^{T}\|

\displaystyle=:A_{1}+A_{2}+A_{3}.

Using Markov’s inequality and Golden-Thompson inequality repeatedly, we get

\displaystyle~~~~{\mathbb{P}}\left(\{\|A_{1}\|>t\}\cap{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq r\cdot e^{-\lambda t}{\mathbb{E}}\left(\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{\lambda}{2n^{2}}[\Delta_{1n}(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-M)\Delta_{1n}^{T}]\right]1_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}}\big|S_{1n}\right\}\right\|^{n}\right).

From triangular inequality, we have

\|\frac{\lambda}{2n^{2}}\Delta_{1n}(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-M)\Delta_{1n}^{T}\|\leq\frac{\lambda}{n^{2}}\left(\|\Delta_{1n}Y_{n}^{T}U\|+\|\Delta_{1n}M^{T}U\|\right).

Under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle~~~~\|\Delta_{1n}Y_{n}^{T}U\|\leq\|S_{1n}Y_{n}^{T}U\|+n\|U^{T}MY_{n}^{T}U\|

\displaystyle\leq(3\alpha+7)p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))\mu rp_{2}+n\sqrt{\mu rp_{2}}p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}.

On the other hand, under the event
{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}_{1}
,

\|\Delta_{1n}M^{T}U\|\leq\|\Delta_{1n}\|\|M^{T}U\|\leq\sqrt{\frac{8(\alpha+1)\mu p_{1}p_{2}^{2}r\log(p)n}{3}}\|M\|_{\infty}\sqrt{p_{1}p_{2}}\|M\|_{\infty}.

As long as
n\geq\frac{8}{3}(\alpha+1)\log(p)
, we have
\|\Delta_{1n}M^{T}U\|\leq n\sqrt{\mu p_{2}r}p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}
and thus

\displaystyle~~~~\frac{\lambda}{2n^{2}}\|\Delta_{1n}(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-M)\Delta_{1n}^{T}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\lambda}{n^{2}}\left((3\alpha+7)p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))\mu rp_{2}+2n\sqrt{\mu rp_{2}}p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}\right).

Therefore for any

\lambda\leq n^{2}\left((3\alpha+7)p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))\mu rp_{2}+2n\sqrt{\mu rp_{2}}p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}\right)^{-1},

we have

\displaystyle~~~~{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{\lambda}{2n^{2}}[\Delta_{1n}(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-M)\Delta_{1n}^{T}]\right]1_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}}\big|S_{1n}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq I_{r}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left[\frac{\lambda^{2}}{4n^{4}}[\Delta_{1n}(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-M)\Delta_{1n}^{T}]^{2}\right]1_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}}\big|S_{1n}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq I_{r}+{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left[\frac{\lambda^{2}}{4n^{4}}[\Delta_{1n}Y_{n}^{T}U+U^{T}Y_{n}\Delta_{1n}^{T}]^{2}\right]1_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}}\big|S_{1n}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq I_{r}+\frac{\lambda^{2}p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}}{4n^{4}}\left[(\mu r+2)\Delta_{1n}\Delta_{1n}^{T}+\text{tr}(\Delta_{1n}\Delta_{1n}^{T})I_{r}\right],

where in the first inequality we use
\exp(A)\leq I+A+A^{2}
for
\|A\|\leq 1
. And notice that
\text{tr}(\Delta_{1n}\Delta_{1n}^{T})\leq r\|\Delta_{1n}\Delta_{1n}^{T}\|
, we obtain

\displaystyle~~~~\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{\lambda}{2n^{2}}[\Delta_{1n}(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-M)\Delta_{1n}^{T}]\right]1_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}}\big|S_{1n}\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 1+\frac{\lambda^{2}p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}\mu r}{2n^{4}}\|\Delta_{1n}\Delta_{1n}^{T}\|

\displaystyle\leq 1+\frac{\lambda^{2}p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}\mu r}{2n^{4}}\frac{8n(\alpha+1)\mu p_{1}p_{2}^{2}r\log(p)}{3}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{4(\alpha+1)\lambda^{2}\mu^{2}r^{2}p_{2}\log(p)}{3n^{3}}(p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2})^{2}.

Therefore we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\{\|A_{1}\|>t\}\cap{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}\right)\leq r\cdot e^{-\lambda t}\exp\left(\frac{4(\alpha+1)\lambda^{2}\mu^{2}r^{2}p_{2}\log(p)}{3n^{2}}(p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2})^{2}\right).

Taking

\displaystyle\lambda=\min\big\{\frac{3n^{2}t}{8(\alpha+1)\mu^{2}r^{2}p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{3}\|M\|_{\infty}^{4}\log(p)},

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}}{(6\alpha+14)p_{1}p_{2}^{2}\mu r\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))},

\displaystyle\frac{n}{4\sqrt{\mu rp_{2}}p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}}\big\}

yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\{\|A_{1}\|>t\}\cap{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}\right)\leq r\cdot\exp

\displaystyle\big(-\min\big\{\frac{3n^{2}t^{2}}{16(\alpha+1)\mu^{2}r^{2}p_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{3}\|M\|_{\infty}^{4}\log(p)},

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}t}{(12\alpha+28)p_{1}p_{2}^{2}\mu r\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}(\frac{n}{p_{2}}+\log(p))},\frac{nt}{8\sqrt{\mu rp_{2}}p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}}\big\}\big).

Now we bound
A_{2}
and
A_{3}
. Due to the symmetry, we shall consider
A_{2}
only. In a similar fashion, we have

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\{\|A_{2}\|>t\}\cap{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}\right)\leq r\cdot\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{\lambda}{2n}(U^{T}M(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-MM^{T}U))\right]1_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}}\right\}\right\|^{n}.

Simple calculations show that

\|\frac{\lambda}{2n}(U^{T}M(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-M)M^{T}U)\|\leq\frac{2\mu rp_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}\lambda}{n}.

So as long as
\lambda\leq\frac{n}{2\mu rp_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}}
,
we have

\displaystyle~~~~\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\exp\left[\frac{\lambda}{2n}(U^{T}M(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-M)M^{T}U)\right]1_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}}\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 1+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left[\frac{\lambda}{2n}(U^{T}M(Y_{n}-M)^{T}U+U^{T}(Y_{n}-M)M^{T}U)\right]^{2}\cdot 1_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}}\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 1+\left\|{\mathbb{E}}\left\{\left[\frac{\lambda}{2n}(U^{T}MY_{n}^{T}U+U^{T}Y_{n}M^{T}U)\right]^{2}\cdot 1_{{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}}\right\}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 1+\frac{\lambda^{2}\mu rp_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{4}}{n^{2}}.

Now take

\lambda=\min\left\{\frac{n}{2\mu rp_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}},\frac{nt}{2\mu rp_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}}\right\}
, then

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}\left(\{\|A_{2}\|>t\}\cap{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}\right)\leq r\cdot\exp\left\{-\min\{\frac{nt}{4\mu rp_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}},\frac{nt^{2}}{4\mu rp_{1}^{2}p_{2}^{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{4}}\}\right\}.

So we have

\displaystyle~~~~{\mathbb{P}}\left\{\|\frac{1}{2n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}(U^{T}X_{i}Y_{j}^{T}U+U^{T}Y_{j}X_{i}^{T}U)-U^{T}MM^{T}U\|>t\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{3}{\mathbb{P}}(\{\|A_{k}\|>t/3\}\cap{\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}})+{\mathbb{P}}({\boldsymbol{\mathcal{E}}}^{c}).

By taking

t=C\alpha^{2}\log^{2}(p)\frac{p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}}{n}\left(\mu rp_{2}^{1/2}+\frac{\mu rp_{2}}{n}+(\frac{\mu rn}{\log^{3}(p)})^{1/2}\right),

we conclude that

{\mathbb{P}}\left\{\|\frac{1}{2n^{2}}\sum_{i,j}(U^{T}X_{i}Y_{j}^{T}U+U^{T}Y_{j}X_{i}^{T}U)-U^{T}MM^{T}U\|>t\right\}\leq 7p^{-\alpha}.

And the proof is finalized by replacing
\alpha
with
\alpha+\log_{p}(7)
and adjusting the constant
C
accordingly.

C.11
Proof of Lemma
36

First denote
Z_{i}=U^{T}(p_{1}p_{2}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{i}}(M)-M)
. Then we have
U^{T}(\frac{p_{1}p_{2}}{n}{\mathcal{P}}_{\Omega}(M)-M)=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}
. Notice that

\|p_{1}p_{2}U^{T}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{i}}(M)\|\leq\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}\sqrt{\mu r}\|M\|_{\infty}
. And this implies that

\|Z_{i}\|\leq 2\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}\sqrt{\mu r}\|M\|_{\infty}.

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}Z_{i}Z_{i}^{T}\leq p_{1}p_{2}U^{T}(\sum_{i,j}M_{ij}^{2}e_{i}e_{i}^{T})U\leq p_{1}p_{2}^{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}I_{r},

and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}Z_{i}^{T}Z_{i}\leq\mu rp_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}I_{p_{2}}.

So
\|{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}Z_{i}^{T}\|\vee\|{\mathbb{E}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}^{T}Z_{i}\|\leq np_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}(\mu r\vee p_{2})
. Using matrix Bernstein inequality and we have with probability exceeding
1-p^{-\alpha}
,

\|U^{T}(p_{1}p_{2}{\mathcal{P}}_{\omega_{i}}(M)-M)\|\leq C\alpha\left(\frac{\sqrt{p_{1}}p_{2}\sqrt{\mu r}\|M\|_{\infty}\log(p)}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p_{1}p_{2}\|M\|_{\infty}^{2}(\mu r\vee p_{2})\log(p)}{n}}\right).
